TITLE: Toga Pack Styling Properties Reference
DESCRIPTION: Comprehensive API documentation for various styling properties used in Toga's Pack layout system, detailing their accepted values, initial states, and behavioral descriptions.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/style/pack.rst#_snippet_13

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Property: margin
Values: <integer> or <tuple> of length 1-4
Description: A shorthand for setting the top, right, bottom and left margin with a single declaration.
- If 1 integer: margin for all sides.
- If 2 integers: first for top/bottom, second for left/right.
- If 3 integers: first for top, second for left/right, third for bottom.
- If 4 integers: top, right, bottom, left respectively.
```

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Property: color
Values: <color>
Initial value: System default
Description: Set the foreground color for the object being rendered. Some objects may not use the value.
```

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Property: background_color
Values: <color> | transparent
Initial value: The platform default background color
Description: Set the background color for the object being rendered. Some objects may not use the value.
```

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Property: text_align
Values: left | right | center | justify
Initial value: left if text_direction is ltr; right if text_direction is rtl
Description: Defines the alignment of text in the object being rendered.
```

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Property: text_direction
Values: rtl | ltr
Initial value: rtl
Description: Defines the natural direction of horizontal content.
```

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Property: font_family
Values: system | serif | sans-serif | cursive | fantasy | monospace | <string>
Initial value: system
Description: The font family to be used.
- system: System-appropriate font.
- serif, sans-serif, cursive, fantasy, monospace: System-defined font matching description.
- <string>: Checked against Font.register; falls back to system font if unresolved.
```

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Property: font_style
Values: normal | italic | oblique
Initial value: normal
Description: The style of the font to be used.
Note: Windows and Android do not support the oblique font style. A request for an oblique font will be interpreted as italic.
```

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Property: font_variant
Values: normal | small_caps
Initial value: normal
Description: The variant of the font to be used.
Note: Windows and Android do not support the small caps variant. A request for a small_caps font will be interpreted as normal.
```

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Property: font_weight
Values: normal | bold
Initial value: normal
Description: The weight of the font to be used.
```

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Property: font_size
Values: <integer>
Initial value: System default
Description: The size of the font to be used, in CSS points.
```

TITLE: Toga App Lifecycle and Event Handlers
DESCRIPTION: Details the lifecycle of a Toga application, including startup, running, and exit events. Event handlers can be defined by subclassing App, assigning values during instantiation, or assigning to an existing instance. Handlers can be asynchronous.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/app.rst#_snippet_3

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
toga.App:
  __init__(self, formal_name: str, app_id: str, project_name: str = None, version: str = None, url: str = None, author: str = None, author_email: str = None, description: str = None, icon: str = None, accessibility: str = None, gui_windows: list = None, document_types: list = None, startup: callable = None, on_exit: callable = None, on_main_window_closed: callable = None, on_startup: callable = None, on_running: callable = None)
    formal_name: The formal name of the application.
    app_id: The unique application ID.
    project_name: The name of the project.
    version: The application version.
    url: The URL of the application.
    author: The author of the application.
    author_email: The email address of the author.
    description: A description of the application.
    icon: Path to the application icon.
    accessibility: Accessibility information.
    gui_windows: List of GUI windows.
    document_types: List of document types the app can manage.
    startup: Method called during app startup (must not be async).
    on_exit: Handler called when the user attempts to exit. Must return a boolean (True to allow exit, False to prevent).
    on_main_window_closed: Handler called when the main window is closed.
    on_startup: Handler called after the app's event loop has started.
    on_running: Handler called as soon as the app's event loop has started.

  startup(self)
    Method called during application startup. Cannot be an asynchronous method.

  on_running(self, app)
    Event handler that occurs as soon as the app's event loop has started.

  on_exit(self, app)
    Event handler that occurs when the user tries to exit. Must return a Boolean value: True if the app is allowed to exit; False otherwise.

  on_startup(self, app)
    Event handler that occurs during application startup.

  on_main_window_closed(self, app)
    Event handler that occurs when the main window is closed.
```

TITLE: Create Table with Tuples
DESCRIPTION: Demonstrates creating a Toga Table widget using a list of tuples for data and a list of strings for headings. It shows how to access data by index and append new rows.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/widgets/table.rst#_snippet_0

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

table = toga.Table(
    headings=["Name", "Age"],
    data=[
        ("Arthur Dent", 42),
        ("Ford Prefect", 37),
        ("Tricia McMillan", 38),
    ]
)

# Get the details of the first item in the data:
print(f"{table.data[0].name} is age {table.data[0].age}")

# Append new data to the table
table.data.append(("Zaphod Beeblebrox", 47))
```

TITLE: Toga API Constructors Design
DESCRIPTION: Details how Toga class constructors handle property initialization, allowing both writable and read-only properties, and positional arguments for single required parameters.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/how-to/topics/api-design.rst#_snippet_2

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Constructors:
  - Writable properties: Initialized via keyword arguments (same names)
  - Read-only properties: Can be accepted (e.g., Widget.id) but not changed later
  - Single required argument: May be passed as a positional argument (e.g., Label text)
```

TITLE: Displaying a Dialog in an Async Context
DESCRIPTION: Demonstrates how to display a dialog (e.g., QuestionDialog) and await its result within an asynchronous method. The dialog is presented as window-modal.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/resources/dialogs.rst#_snippet_0

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
async def my_handler(self, widget, **kwargs):
    ask_a_question = toga.QuestionDialog("Hello!", "Is this OK!")

    if await self.main_window.dialog(ask_a_question):
        print("The user said yes!")
    else:
        print("The user said no.")
```

TITLE: Toga Widget Implementations by Platform
DESCRIPTION: Details the native widget implementations or approaches for various Toga widgets across different operating systems (Cocoa, GTK, iOS, Winforms, Android). This helps in understanding platform-specific challenges and solutions for Toga's cross-platform GUI development.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/roadmap.rst#_snippet_0

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Widget: DateTimeInput
  Description: A widget for selecting a date and a time.
  Platform Implementations:
    - Cocoa: NSDatePicker
    - GTK: Gtk.Calendar + ?
    - iOS: UIDatePicker

Widget: RadioButton
  Description: A set of mutually exclusive options.
  Platform Implementations:
    - Cocoa: (No direct equivalent, use Selection)
    - GTK: (No direct equivalent, use Selection)
    - iOS: (No direct equivalent, use Selection)
    - Winforms: (No direct equivalent, use Selection)
    - Android: (No direct equivalent, use Selection)

Widget: ComboBox
  Description: A free entry text field that provides options.
  Platform Implementations:
    - Cocoa: NSComboBox
    - GTK: Gtk.ComboBox.new_with_model_and_entry
    - iOS: ?
    - Winforms: ComboBox
    - Android: Spinner

Widget: ColorInput
  Description: A widget for selecting a color.
  Platform Implementations:
    - Cocoa: NSColorWell
    - GTK: Gtk.ColorButton or Gtk.ColorSelection
    - iOS: ?
    - Winforms: ?
    - Android: ?

Widget: SearchInput
  Description: A variant of TextField decorated as a search box.
  Platform Implementations:
    - Cocoa: NSSearchField
    - GTK: Gtk.Entry
    - iOS: UISearchBar?
    - Winforms: ?
    - Android: ?

View: VideoView
  Description: Display a video.
  Platform Implementations:
    - Cocoa: AVPlayerView
    - GTK: Custom integration with GStreamer
    - iOS: MPMoviePlayerController
    - Winforms: ?
    - Android: ?

View: PDFView
  Description: Display a PDF document.
  Platform Implementations:
    - Cocoa: PDFView
    - GTK: ?
    - iOS: Integration with QuickLook?
    - Winforms: ?
    - Android: ?

Container Widget: FormContainer
  Description: A layout for a "key/value" or "label/widget" form.
  Platform Implementations:
    - Cocoa: NSForm, or NSView with pre-set constraints.
    - GTK: 
    - iOS:
    - Winforms: ?
    - Android: ?

Container Widget: NavigationContainer
  Description: A container view that holds a navigable tree of sub-views.
  Platform Implementations:
    - Cocoa: No native control
    - GTK: No native control; Gtk.HeaderBar in 3.10+
    - iOS: UINavigationBar + NavigationController
    - Winforms: ?
    - Android: ?
```

TITLE: Menu Status Icon with Commands
DESCRIPTION: Illustrates the creation and usage of a menu status icon in Toga, allowing for the display of commands in a context menu. It shows how to associate commands with a menu status icon's group, create submenus, and add these commands to the application's status icon command set. It also mentions how to manage default commands.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/resources/statusicons.rst#_snippet_1

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

# Create a MenuStatusIcon
status_icon = toga.MenuStatusIcon(icon="icons/blue")

# Create some commands that are associated with the menu status icon's group.
def callback(sender, **kwargs):
    print("Command activated")

cmd1 = toga.Command(
    callback,
    text='Example command',
    group=status_icon,
)

# Create a sub-group of the status icon. This will appear as a submenu.
stuff_group = toga.Group('Stuff', parent=status_icon)

cmd2 = toga.Command(
    callback,
    text='Stuff sub-command',
    group=stuff_group
)

# Add the status icon to the app
app.status_icons.add(status_icon)

# Add the commands to the status icons command set
app.status_icons.commands.add(cmd1, cmd2)
```

    # app_log: Path to the directory for application-specific log files.
    # app_cache: Path to the directory for application-specific cache files.
    # app_data: Path to the directory for application-specific user data files.

TITLE: Toga Event Loop Guarantee on Startup
DESCRIPTION: Guarantees the event loop is running when the app's `startup()` method is invoked, fixing issues on macOS and Windows.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_62

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

class EventLoopStartupApp(toga.App):
    def startup(self):
        # The event loop is guaranteed to be running here.
        print("App startup: Event loop is active.")
        self.main_window = toga.MainWindow(title=self.formal_name)
        self.main_window.show()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    app = EventLoopStartupApp('EventLoopApp', 'org.example.eventloopapp')
    app.main_loop()

```

App Paths:

TITLE: Toolbar Creation Restricted to Toga MainWindow
DESCRIPTION: Toolbars can no longer be created directly on a generic `toga.Window` instance. They are now exclusively supported and can only be added to `MainWindow` or its subclasses. This change streamlines toolbar management, associating them primarily with the application's main window.

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
toga.Window:
  toolbar:
    Status: Cannot be created directly on a generic Window instance.

toga.MainWindow:
  toolbar:
    Description: Toolbars can only be added to MainWindow or its subclasses.
```

TITLE: Update Selection Widget Handler and Data Source APIs
DESCRIPTION: The 'on_select' handler on the 'Selection' widget is renamed to 'on_change' for consistency. The internal '_notify()' method on data sources is now public as 'notify()'. The 'prepend()' method has been removed from 'ListSource' and 'TreeSource', with 'insert(0, ...)' as the replacement. The 'insert()' and 'append()' methods on 'ListSource' and 'TreeSource' now require arguments to be combined into a single tuple or dictionary, aligning with the '__setitem__()' API.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/background/project/releases.rst#_snippet_28

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Selection Widget:
  Handler 'on_select': renamed to 'on_change'.
Data Sources (general):
  Method '_notify()': renamed to 'notify()'. Now a public API.
ListSource, TreeSource:
  Method 'prepend()': removed. Use 'insert(0, ...)'.
  Methods 'insert()', 'append()':
    Argument signature changed. Now accept a single tuple or dictionary, matching '__setitem__()'.
    Previously accepted variable positional/keyword arguments.
```

TITLE: Simple Status Icon Creation and Management
DESCRIPTION: Demonstrates how to create, configure, and manage simple status icons in a Toga application. This includes setting default or explicit properties like ID, text, and icon, and handling press events. It also shows how to add, retrieve by index or ID, and remove status icons from the application.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/resources/statusicons.rst#_snippet_0

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

# Define a status icon that uses default values for icon and tooltip,
# and doesn't respond to mouse clicks.
status_icon_1 = toga.SimpleStatusIcon()

# Define a second status icon that provides explicit values for the id, icon and
# tooltip, and responds to mouse clicks.
def my_handler(widget, **kwargs):
    print("Second status icon pressed!")

status_icon_2 = toga.SimpleStatusIcon(
    id="second",
    text="Hello!",
    icon="icons/red",
    on_press=my_handler
)

# Add both status icons to the app
app.status_icons.add(status_icon_1, status_icon_2)

# Change the icon of the first status icon, retrieved by index:
app.status_icons[0].icon = "icons/green"

# Change the icon of the second status icon, retrieved by id:
app.status_icons["second"].icon = "icons/blue"

# Remove the first status icon from the app:
app.status_icons.remove(status_icon_1)
```

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

class MyApp(toga.App):
    ...
    async def time_for_a_selfie(self, widget, **kwargs):
        photo = await self.camera.take_photo()
```

TITLE: Toga Canvas Widget Tutorial
DESCRIPTION: This tutorial demonstrates how to use the Canvas widget to draw lines and shapes programmatically within your Toga application.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/tutorial/index.rst#_snippet_4

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from toga import App, Canvas

class DrawingApp(App):
    def startup(self):
        canvas = Canvas()
        canvas.on_draw = self.draw_content
        self.main_window.content = canvas

    def draw_content(self, widget):
        # Example: Draw a line
        widget.stroke_color = 'red'
        widget.stroke_width = 2
        widget.move_to(10, 10)
        widget.line_to(100, 100)
        widget.stroke()

        # Example: Draw a rectangle
        widget.fill_color = 'blue'
        widget.fill_style = 'solid'
        widget.rect(120, 120, 80, 50)

if __name__ == '__main__':
    app = DrawingApp()
    app.main_loop()

```

TITLE: Deprecation of `BaseStyle.apply()` Multiple Arguments
DESCRIPTION: The ability to supply multiple arguments directly to `BaseStyle.apply()` (and `Pack.apply()`) is deprecated. Users should now use the `batch_apply()` context manager for applying multiple style properties simultaneously.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_7

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
# Deprecated usage:
# my_widget.style.apply(padding=10, margin=5)

# Recommended usage:
with my_widget.batch_apply():
    my_widget.style.padding = 10
    my_widget.style.margin = 5

```

TITLE: Toga Label Widget API Reference
DESCRIPTION: Provides the API documentation for the toga.Label class, detailing its constructor and available attributes for customization. This reference is crucial for understanding how to configure and use the Label widget effectively.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/widgets/label.rst#_snippet_1

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
toga.Label:
  __init__(text: str, id: str = None, style: Style = None, 
           factory: 'WidgetFactory' = None, 
           accessKey: str = None, 
           container: 'Widget' = None, 
           content: 'Widget' = None, 
           enabled: bool = True, 
           hidden: bool = False, 
           id_name: str = None, 
           key: str = None, 
           label: str = None, 
           name: str = None, 
           on_change: callable = None, 
           on_press: callable = None, 
           on_release: callable = None, 
           parent: 'Widget' = None, 
           style: Style = None, 
           tooltip: str = None, 
           vertical: bool = False, 
           widget: 'Widget' = None, 
           window: 'Window' = None)
    text: The text to be displayed by the label.
    id: A unique identifier for the widget.
    style: A Toga style object to customize the widget's appearance.
    accessKey: A keyboard shortcut to activate the widget.
    container: The container widget that this widget belongs to.
    content: The content of the widget.
    enabled: Whether the widget is enabled or disabled.
    hidden: Whether the widget is hidden or visible.
    id_name: An alias for the id parameter.
    key: A unique key for the widget.
    label: A label associated with the widget.
    name: The name of the widget.
    on_change: A callback function to be executed when the widget's value changes.
    on_press: A callback function to be executed when the widget is pressed.
    on_release: A callback function to be executed when the widget is released.
    parent: The parent widget of this widget.
    tooltip: Text to display when the mouse hovers over the widget.
    vertical: Whether the widget should be laid out vertically.
    widget: The underlying native widget.
    window: The window that contains this widget.

Notes:
  - Winforms does not support a text alignment value of ``JUSTIFIED``. If this alignment value is used, the label will default to left alignment.
```

TITLE: Managing OptionContainer Content by OptionItem Reference
DESCRIPTION: Explains how to manage OptionContainer content using direct references to OptionItem objects. This includes inserting, setting the current tab, and deleting tabs via these references.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/containers/optioncontainer.rst#_snippet_4

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
# Get a reference to the "Pasta" tab
pasta_tab = container.content["Pasta"]
# Insert content at the index currently occupied by the pasta tab
container.content.insert(pasta_tab, "Soup", toga.Box())
# Make the pasta tab the currently active tab
container.current_tab = pasta_tab
# Delete the pasta tab
del container.content[pasta_tab]
```

TITLE: CommandSet Interface Enhancements
DESCRIPTION: CommandSet now provides full set and dictionary interfaces for adding and removing commands.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_68

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from toga.command import CommandSet, Command

cmd_set = CommandSet()
cmd = Command('My Command', on_execute=lambda widget: print('Command executed'))

# Add command using [] notation
cmd_set['cmd_id'] = cmd

# Remove command using set-like discard
cmd_set.discard(cmd)

# Remove command using dictionary-like del
del cmd_set['cmd_id']
```

TITLE: System Requirements for Linux

- Distribution-specific package names:
  - Ubuntu/Debian: gir1.2-geoclue-2.0
  - Fedora: geoclue2-libs
  - Arch/Manjaro: geoclue
  - OpenSUSE Tumbleweed: geoclue2 typelib(geoclue2)
  - FreeBSD: geoclue
- The GeoClue service must be enabled in the system's privacy settings.

TITLE: Deprecated Toga Window Dialog APIs and New Dialog Class Usage
DESCRIPTION: The direct dialog APIs on `Window` (e.g., `info_dialog()`, `question_dialog()`) have been deprecated. Developers should now create instances of specific `Dialog` classes (e.g., `InfoDialog`, `QuestionDialog`) and pass that instance to the `window.dialog()` method for displaying dialogs. This promotes a more consistent and extensible dialog management pattern.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/background/project/releases.rst#_snippet_16

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
toga.Window:
  info_dialog(title: str, message: str):
    Status: Deprecated
    Replacement: Use toga.InfoDialog instance with window.dialog().
  question_dialog(title: str, message: str):
    Status: Deprecated
    Replacement: Use toga.QuestionDialog instance with window.dialog().
  # ... other similar dialog methods are also deprecated
  dialog(dialog_instance: toga.Dialog):
    Description: Displays a dialog instance on the window.
    Parameters:
      dialog_instance: An instance of a toga.Dialog subclass (e.g., InfoDialog, QuestionDialog, ConfirmDialog).

toga.InfoDialog:
  __init__(title: str, message: str):
    Description: Constructor for an informational dialog.

toga.QuestionDialog:
  __init__(title: str, message: str):
    Description: Constructor for a question dialog, typically returning True/False.
```

TITLE: Toga Table API Documentation
DESCRIPTION: Provides API documentation for the toga.Table class, including its constructor, data access, and platform-specific notes.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/widgets/table.rst#_snippet_3

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
class toga.Table:
    """A widget for displaying columns of tabular data."""

    def __init__(self,
            headings,
            data=None,
            accessors=None,
            on_select=None,
            on_activate=None,
            multi_select=False,
            missing_value=''):
        """Initialize a Table widget.

        Args:
            headings (list): A list of strings for the column headings.
            data (list, optional): A list of data rows. Each row can be a tuple or a dictionary.
                                   Defaults to None.
            accessors (dict, optional): A dictionary mapping heading strings to the attribute names
                                        used to access data in each row. Defaults to None.
            on_select (callable, optional): A function to be called when a row is selected.
                                            Defaults to None.
            on_activate (callable, optional): A function to be called when a row is activated.
                                              Defaults to None.
            multi_select (bool, optional): Whether multiple rows can be selected. Defaults to False.
            missing_value (str, optional): The value to display for missing data. Defaults to ''.

        Platform Notes:
            - Widgets in cells is a beta API, currently only supported on macOS.
            - macOS does not support changing the font used to render table content.
            - On Winforms, icons are only supported in the first column.
            - On Android, icons are not supported.
            - The Android implementation is not scalable beyond ~1,000 cells.
        """
        pass

    @property
    def data(self):
        """Get or set the data for the table."""
        pass

    @property
    def selected(self):
        """Get the currently selected row(s)."""
        pass

    def scroll_to_row(self, row):
        """Scroll the table to ensure the specified row is visible."""
        pass

protocol toga.widgets.table.OnSelectHandler:
    """Handler for row selection events."""
    def __call__(self, widget, row):
        """Called when a row is selected.

        Args:
            widget (Table): The table widget that triggered the event.
            row (object): The selected row object.
        """
        pass

protocol toga.widgets.table.OnActivateHandler:
    """Handler for row activation events."""
    def __call__(self, widget, row):
        """Called when a row is activated.

        Args:
            widget (Table): The table widget that triggered the event.
            row (object): The activated row object.
        """
        pass

```

TITLE: Toga Capability: Cloud Data Access (Roadmap)

Cloud Data Access:
  Description: Traditional apps store all their files locally; however, using cloud services or network resources is increasingly common, especially in mobile apps. However, accessing cloud-based files can be very complicated; Toga is in a position to provide a file-like API that abstracts accessing these APIs.

TITLE: Enhanced CommandSet Interface in Toga
DESCRIPTION: The `CommandSet` class now exposes a full set and dictionary interface, allowing commands to be added, removed, and managed using familiar `[]` notation, `remove()`, `discard()`, `pop()`, and `del` operations. This provides more flexible and intuitive command management.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/background/project/releases.rst#_snippet_11

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
toga.CommandSet:
  __getitem__(command_id: str):
    Description: Access a command by its ID.
  __setitem__(command_id: str, command_instance: toga.Command):
    Description: Add a command by ID.
  remove(command_instance: toga.Command):
    Description: Remove a command instance (set-like operation).
  discard(command_instance: toga.Command):
    Description: Discard a command instance (set-like operation).
  pop(command_id: str):
    Description: Remove and return a command by ID (dictionary-like operation).
  __delitem__(command_id: str):
    Description: Delete a command by ID (dictionary-like operation).
```

TITLE: Update Toga Image Constructor to use `src` argument

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/background/project/releases.rst#_snippet_19

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
toga.Image(src)
  src: Union[str, bytes, PIL.Image.Image, Any]
    The source for the image. Can be a file path, raw bytes, a Pillow (PIL) Image object, or a native platform image representation.
  Deprecated arguments: path, data
```

```

TITLE: Toga Key Class Reference

class toga.Key:
    """Symbolic representation of keys used for keyboard shortcuts."""

    # Constants for standard keys (e.g., Key.A, Key.B, Key.SPACE, Key.ENTER)
    # ...

    # Generic modifier constants:
    MOD_1: Any
        """The primary modifier key (e.g., Control on Linux/Windows, Command on macOS)."""
    MOD_2: Any
        """The secondary modifier key (e.g., Alt on Linux/Windows, Option on macOS)."""
    MOD_3: Any
        """The tertiary modifier key (e.g., Windows/Meta on Linux, Control on macOS)."""

    # Key combinations can be expressed by combining multiple Key values with the '+' operator.
    # Example: Key.MOD_1 + Key.SHIFT + Key.A

TITLE: DocumentWindow Class Reference
DESCRIPTION: Provides the reference documentation for the DocumentWindow class, detailing its purpose and usage within document-based applications.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/documentwindow.rst#_snippet_0

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
toga.DocumentWindow
  A window that can be used as the main interface to a document-based app.

  Usage:
    A DocumentWindow is the same as a :any:`toga.MainWindow`, except that it is bound to
    a :any:`toga.Document` instance, exposed as the :any:`toga.DocumentWindow.doc`
    attribute.

    Instances of :any:`toga.DocumentWindow` should be created as part of the
    :meth:`~toga.Document.create()` method of an implementation of :any:`toga.Document`.
```

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
NavigationContainer:
  Status: New container widget
  Description: A container view that holds a navigable tree of sub-views. Essentially a view that has a "back" button to return to the previous view in a hierarchy. Example of use: Top level navigation in the macOS System Preferences panel.
  Native Controls:
    Cocoa: No native control
    GTK: No native control; Gtk.HeaderBar in 3.10+
    iOS: UINavigationBar + NavigationController
    Winforms: ?
    Android: ?
```

TITLE: Selection with Dictionaries and Accessor
DESCRIPTION: Illustrates using a list of dictionaries with an accessor to display specific attributes. Shows how to set the value using a dictionary object or by finding an item, and how to access attributes of the selected item.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/widgets/selection.rst#_snippet_1

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

selection = toga.Selection(
    items=[
        {"name": "Alice", "age": 37},
        {"name": "Bob", "age": 42},
        {"name": "Charlie", "age": 24},
    ],
    accessor="name",
)

# Select Bob explicitly
selection.value = selection.items[1]

# What is the age of the currently selected person? This will print 42
print(f"Age of currently selected person: {selection.value.age}")

# Select Charlie by searching
selection.value = selection.items.find(name="Charlie")
```

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/roadmap.rst#_snippet_1

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Widget: Table
  Description: Represents table-like data.
  Platform Considerations:
    - Mobile: No native 'Table' widget; use DetailedList for table-like data.

Widget: Tree
  Description: Represents hierarchical data.
  Platform Considerations:
    - Mobile: No native 'Tree' widget; similar approach to Table.
    - Windows: Native widget lacks multi-column support; requires special casing or composite widget.
```

CODE:
```
Source:
  __init__(self, listener=None)
    listener: An optional Listener object to receive notifications about data changes.

  add_listener(self, listener)
    Adds a listener to the data source.

  remove_listener(self, listener)
    Removes a listener from the data source.

  notify(self, **kwargs)
    Notifies all registered listeners about a change, passing keyword arguments to the handler.
```

from travertino.colors import color, rgb

# Reference a pre-defined color
color('RebeccaPurple')
```

TITLE: Toga App with Document Types
DESCRIPTION: Shows how to configure a Toga application to support custom document types. The `document_types` parameter in the `toga.App` constructor registers the document classes, with the first one listed becoming the default. This automatically adds file management commands like New, Open, and Save.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/resources/document.rst#_snippet_1

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

class ExampleApp(toga.App):
    def startup(self):
        # The app does not have a single main window
        self.main_window = None

app = ExampleApp(
    "Document App",
    "com.example.documentapp",
    document_types=[ExampleDocument, OtherDocument]
)

app.main_loop()
```

  Status: New widget
  Description: A set of mutually exclusive options. Functionally, the use case of "select one from a list of options" can be met with a :class:`~toga.Selection`; however, from a UI design perspective, a radio button is a common design pattern.

TITLE: General Widgets
DESCRIPTION: Provides a collection of reusable UI elements for building graphical interfaces in Toga applications. This includes input controls, display elements, and layout aids.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/index.rst#_snippet_1

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
ActivityIndicator:
  Description: A small animated indicator showing activity on a task of indeterminate length, usually rendered as a "spinner" animation.
  Reference: </reference/api/widgets/activityindicator>

Button:
  Description: A button that can be pressed or clicked.
  Reference: </reference/api/widgets/button>

Canvas:
  Description: A drawing area for 2D vector graphics.
  Reference: </reference/api/widgets/canvas>

DateInput:
  Description: A widget to select a calendar date.
  Reference: </reference/api/widgets/dateinput>

DetailedList:
  Description: An ordered list of content where each item has an icon, a main heading, and a line of supplementary text.
  Reference: </reference/api/widgets/detailedlist>

Divider:
  Description: A separator used to visually distinguish two sections of content in a layout.
  Reference: </reference/api/widgets/divider>

ImageView:
  Description: Image Viewer.
  Reference: </reference/api/widgets/imageview>

Label:
  Description: A text label for annotating forms or interfaces.
  Reference: </reference/api/widgets/label>

MapView:
  Description: A zoomable map that can be annotated with location pins.
  Reference: </reference/api/widgets/mapview>

MultilineTextInput:
  Description: A scrollable panel that allows for the display and editing of multiple lines of text.
  Reference: </reference/api/widgets/multilinetextinput>

NumberInput:
  Description: A text input that is limited to numeric input.
  Reference: </reference/api/widgets/numberinput>

PasswordInput:
  Description: A widget to allow the entry of a password. Any value typed by the user will be obscured, allowing the user to see the number of characters they have typed, but not the actual characters.
  Reference: </reference/api/widgets/passwordinput>

ProgressBar:
  Description: A horizontal bar to visualize task progress. The task being monitored can be of known or indeterminate length.
  Reference: </reference/api/widgets/progressbar>

Selection:
  Description: A widget to select an single option from a list of alternatives.
  Reference: </reference/api/widgets/selection>

Slider:
  Description: A widget for selecting a value within a range. The range is shown as a horizontal line, and the selected value is shown as a draggable marker.
  Reference: </reference/api/widgets/slider>

Switch:
  Description: A clickable button with two stable states: True (on, checked); and False (off, unchecked). The button has a text label.
  Reference: </reference/api/widgets/switch>

Table:
  Description: A widget for displaying columns of tabular data.
  Reference: </reference/api/widgets/table>

TextInput:
  Description: A widget for the display and editing of a single line of text.
  Reference: </reference/api/widgets/textinput>

TimeInput:
  Description: A widget to select a clock time.
  Reference: </reference/api/widgets/timeinput>
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Toga Color Constructors (RGB/HSL)
DESCRIPTION: Explains the behavior of Toga's rgb and hsl constructors regarding out-of-range values and type conversions. Out-of-range values are now clipped or wrapped instead of causing errors. Color components are converted to consistent types: integers for red, blue, green, and hue; and floats for saturation, lightness, and alpha.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/changes/3611.removal.2.rst#_snippet_0

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from toga.colors import rgb, hsl

# Example of clipping for RGB
red_clipped = rgb(256, 0, 0) # Will be clipped to 255
print(red_clipped.red) # Output: 255

# Example of wrapping for HSL hue
hsl_wrapped = hsl(400, 0.5, 0.5) # Hue 400 wraps to 40
print(hsl_wrapped.hue) # Output: 40

# Example of type conversion
color = rgb(100, 150.5, 200)
print(f"Red: {color.red} (type: {type(color.red)})")
print(f"Green: {color.green} (type: {type(color.green)})")
print(f"Blue: {color.blue} (type: {type(color.blue)})")

color_hsl = hsl(120, 0.75, 0.6, 0.9)
print(f"Hue: {color_hsl.hue} (type: {type(color_hsl.hue)})")
print(f"Saturation: {color_hsl.saturation} (type: {type(color_hsl.saturation)})")
print(f"Lightness: {color_hsl.lightness} (type: {type(color_hsl.lightness)})")
print(f"Alpha: {color_hsl.alpha} (type: {type(color_hsl.alpha)})")
```

TITLE: ListSource and TreeSource Method Changes in Toga

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_176

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from toga.sources import ListSource

# Assuming 'source' is an instance of ListSource
# source = ListSource()

# Removed prepend method:
# source.prepend('item1', 'item2')

# New insert method:
# source.insert(0, 'item1')

# Modified append method:
# Previous: source.append(column1='value1', column2='value2')
# New: source.append({'column1': 'value1', 'column2': 'value2'})
```

TITLE: Toga API Properties Design
DESCRIPTION: Explains Toga's approach to exposing object state via properties, adhering to Postel's Law for type handling.

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Properties:
  - Access: widget.property (not getter/setter methods)
  - Postel's Law:
    - Accepts any object when set
    - Always returns a string when retrieved (e.g., widget.text)
```

TITLE: toga.sources.ListSource Class Reference
DESCRIPTION: The `ListSource` class provides an ordered list abstraction for application data, offering a Python `list`-like API. It manages `Row` objects and supports operations such as insertion, removal, indexing, and item retrieval. It also generates notifications for data changes, making it suitable for dynamic GUI widgets.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/resources/sources/list_source.rst#_snippet_2

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
class toga.sources.ListSource
  __len__()
    Returns the number of items in the ListSource.
  __getitem__(index)
    Retrieves an item by index, allowing list-like access (e.g., `source[0]`).
  __setitem__(index, value)
    Sets an item at a given index, allowing list-like assignment (e.g., `source[0] = new_value`).
  __delitem__(index)
    Deletes an item at a given index, allowing list-like deletion (e.g., `del source[0]`).
```

TITLE: Debug Layout Colors Demo App
DESCRIPTION: A sample Toga application demonstrating the debug layout colors tool. It creates a series of nested boxes, each with a margin, to visualize the distinct colors assigned by the debugging tool. The tool is enabled directly in the code.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/topics/debugging.rst#_snippet_1

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

toga.Widget.DEBUG_LAYOUT_ENABLED = True

class DebugLayoutColors(toga.App):
    def startup(self):
        main_box = toga.Box()
        prev_box = main_box
        for _ in range(1, 12):
            new_box = toga.Box(margin=30)
            prev_box.add(new_box)
            prev_box = new_box

        self.main_window = toga.MainWindow(content=main_box)
        self.main_window.show()

def main():
    return DebugLayoutColors("Debug Layout Colors Demo", "org.beeware.toga.debug.layout")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main().main_loop()
```

TITLE: Update Toga OptionContainer `insert` and `append` methods
DESCRIPTION: When inserting or appending a tab to an `OptionContainer`, the `enabled` argument must now be provided as a keyword argument. Additionally, the first argument has been renamed from `text` to `text_or_item` and should generally be passed positionally.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/background/project/releases.rst#_snippet_20

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
toga.OptionContainer.insert(text_or_item, index, *, enabled=True)
  text_or_item: Union[str, toga.OptionItem]
    The text label for the new tab, or a toga.OptionItem object.
  index: int
    The position at which to insert the new tab.
  enabled: bool (keyword-only)
    If True, the tab is enabled; otherwise, it is disabled. Defaults to True.

toga.OptionContainer.append(text_or_item, *, enabled=True)
  text_or_item: Union[str, toga.OptionItem]
    The text label for the new tab, or a toga.OptionItem object.
  enabled: bool (keyword-only)
    If True, the tab is enabled; otherwise, it is disabled. Defaults to True.
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Travertino Color API
DESCRIPTION: Provides an overview of the Travertino color classes, including their constructors, properties, and how they handle alpha channels, aligning with CSS color specifications.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/changes/3611.removal.1.rst#_snippet_1

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Travertino Color Classes:

rgb(red, green, blue, alpha=1.0)
  - Creates an RGB color object.
  - Parameters:
    - red (int): Red component (0-255).
    - green (int): Green component (0-255).
    - blue (int): Blue component (0-255).
    - alpha (float, optional): Alpha component (0.0-1.0). Defaults to 1.0.
  - Properties:
    - red: Red component.
    - green: Green component.
    - blue: Blue component.
    - alpha: Alpha component.

hsl(hue, saturation, lightness, alpha=1.0)
  - Creates an HSL color object.
  - Parameters:
    - hue (int): Hue component (0-360).
    - saturation (float): Saturation component (0.0-1.0).
    - lightness (float): Lightness component (0.0-1.0).
    - alpha (float, optional): Alpha component (0.0-1.0). Defaults to 1.0.
  - Properties:
    - hue: Hue component.
    - saturation: Saturation component.
    - lightness: Lightness component.
    - alpha: Alpha component.

rgba = rgb
  - Alias for the rgb class, preserving alpha channel information.

hsla = hsl
  - Alias for the hsl class, preserving alpha channel information.

Example Usage:
  opaque_red = rgb(255, 0, 0)
  transparent_blue = rgba(0, 0, 255, 0.5)
  print(f"Opaque Red Alpha: {opaque_red.alpha}")
  print(f"Transparent Blue Alpha: {transparent_blue.alpha}")
```

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/how-to/topics/api-design.rst#_snippet_4

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Common Names:
  - Simple value control (e.g., TextInput, Switch):
    - Property: 'value' (e.g., str, bool)
    - Event: 'on_change'
  - Non-editable caption (e.g., Button, Switch):
    - Property: 'text'
  - Number ranges:
    - Properties: 'min', 'max'
```

TITLE: Linux Location Permissions
DESCRIPTION: Explains Linux location permission handling for sandboxed and non-sandboxed applications. Sandboxed apps use XDG Portal API, while non-sandboxed apps have limitations.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/hardware/location.rst#_snippet_7

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Linux Location Permissions:
Non-sandboxed applications lack reliable permission controls. Sandboxed applications (e.g., Flatpak) use the XDG Portal Location API for coarse-grained permissions, allowing users to disallow location access per-app.

Users should be aware of potential privacy limitations for non-sandboxed applications.
```

TITLE: Displaying a Dialog in a Synchronous Context
DESCRIPTION: Shows how to display a dialog from a synchronous event handler by creating an asyncio task and adding a callback for when the dialog is dismissed. This allows handling the dialog's result asynchronously.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/resources/dialogs.rst#_snippet_1

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import asyncio

def my_sync_handler(self, widget, **kwargs):
    ask_a_question = toga.QuestionDialog("Hello!", "Is this OK!")

    task = asyncio.create_task(self.main_window.dialog(ask_a_question))
    task.add_done_callback(self.dialog_dismissed)
    print("Dialog has been created")

def dialog_dismissed(self, task):
    if task.result():
        print("The user said yes!")
    else:
        print("The user said no.")
```

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
toga.Window:
  Resize Behavior:
    - Layout of window content is not triggered if window size remains unchanged after a resize request.
```

TITLE: Toga Pack.reapply() Deprecation
DESCRIPTION: The `reapply()` method in Travertino's `BaseStyle` (and consequently Toga's `Pack`) is now deprecated. The recommended approach is to call `.apply()` without any arguments. While user code rarely calls this directly, older Toga versions (<= 0.4.8) used it extensively, potentially leading to `DeprecationWarning` if Travertino is updated without Toga.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_40

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
class BaseStyle:
    def reapply(self):
        # Deprecated: Use apply() instead
        # ...
        pass

    def apply(self):
        # ...
        pass

class Pack(BaseStyle):
    pass

# Deprecated usage:
# pack_instance.reapply()

# Recommended usage:
# pack_instance.apply()
```

TITLE: Running the Application in Development Mode
DESCRIPTION: This command uses `briefcase` to run the Positron application in development mode. This mode typically includes features like live reloading and debugging, which are useful during active development.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/examples/positron-django/README.rst#_snippet_2

LANGUAGE: Shell

(venv) $ briefcase dev
```

TITLE: Image Loading from URLs in Toga
DESCRIPTION: Images and ImageViews no longer support direct URL loading. Developers must use background tasks to fetch image data asynchronously and then update the ImageView.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_177

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from toga.sources import Image
from toga.widgets import ImageView

# Asynchronous task to load image data
def load_image_data(url):
    # ... fetch image data from url ...
    return image_data

# In your app:
image_data = load_image_data("http://example.com/image.png")
my_image = Image(data=image_data)
my_imageView = ImageView(image=my_image)

```

TITLE: Python Button Usage
DESCRIPTION: Demonstrates how to create and use a Toga Button in Python, including setting text, icons, and press event handlers.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/widgets/button.rst#_snippet_0

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

def my_callback(button):
    # handle event
    pass

button = toga.Button("Click me", on_press=my_callback)

icon_button = toga.Button(icon=toga.Icon("resources/my_icon"), on_press=my_callback)
```

TITLE: Toga WebView Class API Reference
DESCRIPTION: API documentation for the `toga.WebView` class, which represents an embedded web browser widget. This reference details its constructor, properties, and methods for loading and managing web content within a Toga application.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/widgets/webview.rst#_snippet_2

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
toga.WebView
  __init__()
    Initializes a new WebView widget.
  url: str
    The URL currently loaded in the webview. Can be set to load a new URL.
  load_url(url: str) -> None (async)
    Asynchronously loads a URL into the webview and waits for the page to complete loading.
    Parameters:
      url (str): The URL to load.
  set_content(base_url: str, html_content: str) -> None
    Loads static HTML content into the webview.
    Parameters:
      base_url (str): The base URL for resolving relative paths within the HTML content.
      html_content (str): The HTML content string to display.
```

TITLE: DetailedList User Actions and Data Handling
DESCRIPTION: Enhancements to the DetailedList widget, allowing it to respond to 'primary' and 'secondary' user actions (e.g., swipes). It also includes improved handling for rows that may not provide required data, with customizable accessors.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_148

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from toga.sources import DetailedList

# Example of customizing accessors and handling actions
class MyDetailedList(DetailedList):
    def __init__(self, ...):
        super().__init__(...
        # Define custom accessors if needed
        self.accessor = 'my_custom_data'

    def on_primary_action(self, widget, row):
        print(f"Primary action on row: {row}")

    def on_secondary_action(self, widget, row):
        print(f"Secondary action on row: {row}")

    def get_value_for_row(self, row, property_name):
        # Provide a default value if data is missing
        return super().get_value_for_row(row, property_name) or 'N/A'

```

TITLE: Button Widget API Documentation
DESCRIPTION: Provides complete API documentation for the Toga Button widget, ensuring all functionalities and parameters are clearly defined. This enhances developer understanding and usability.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_199

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Button:
  __init__(text, on_press=None, **kwargs)
    text: The text to display on the button.
    on_press: A function to be called when the button is pressed.
    **kwargs: Additional keyword arguments for widget customization.

  Methods:
    enable()
      Enables the button, allowing user interaction.
    disable()
      Disables the button, preventing user interaction.
    focus()
      Sets the input focus to this widget.
    refresh()
      Requests a redraw of the widget.

  Properties:
    text (str): The text displayed on the button.
    enabled (bool): Whether the button is enabled or disabled.
    style (Style): The styling information for the widget.
```

        # Create file in file system and add to data tree
        # ...
        self.notify('insert', parent=parent_node, item=new_node)

    # Implement other methods as needed, ensuring notifications are sent

```

TITLE: Bracket Notation for Pack.margin
DESCRIPTION: The `Pack.margin` property (and its alias `padding`) can now be accessed using bracket notation, e.g., `style["margin"]`. This provides a more consistent way to access style properties.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_19

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from toga.style import Pack

style = Pack(margin_top=5, margin_left=10)

# Accessing margin:
margin_value = style["margin"]
# Note: This would typically apply to the overall margin if set, 
# or could be used for specific properties if the API supported it directly.
# The primary change is enabling access like style['margin'] for the property itself.
```

TITLE: Creating and Modifying OptionContainer Tabs (OptionItem Format)
DESCRIPTION: Illustrates creating an OptionContainer using OptionItem objects, which provides more explicit control over tab properties like icons and enabled status. It also shows how to add a disabled tab.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/containers/optioncontainer.rst#_snippet_1

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

pizza = toga.Box()
pasta = toga.Box()

# Create 2 initial tabs; one with an icon, and one without.
container = toga.OptionContainer(
    content=[
      toga.OptionItem("Pizza", pizza),
      toga.OptionItem("Pasta", pasta, icon=toga.Icon("pasta"))
    ]
)

# Add another tab of content, initially disabled, without an icon.
salad = toga.Box()
container.content.append(toga.OptionItem("Salad", salad, enabled=False))
```

TITLE: Toga System Tray Integration
DESCRIPTION: Allows Toga applications to add items to the system tray, providing quick access to app functions or status information.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_46

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

class TrayApp(toga.App):
    def startup(self):
        # Create and add a system tray icon
        self.main_window = toga.MainWindow(title=self.formal_name)
        self.main_window.show()

        # Example: Add a menu item to the system tray
        tray_menu = [
            toga.MenuItem('Show Window', on_press=self.show_window),
            toga.MenuItem('Exit', on_press=self.exit_app)
        ]
        self.add_system_tray_menu(tray_menu)

    def show_window(self, widget):
        self.main_window.show()

    def exit_app(self, widget):
        self.exit()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    app = TrayApp('TrayApp', 'org.example.trayapp')
    app.main_loop()

```

TITLE: Toga Frozen Binary Startup Fix
DESCRIPTION: Prevents crashes on startup for apps bundled as standalone frozen binaries (e.g., PyInstaller on POSIX) when resolving the app icon.

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

class FrozenBinaryApp(toga.App):
    def startup(self):
        # App icon resolution should now work correctly for frozen binaries
        self.main_window = toga.MainWindow(title=self.formal_name)
        self.main_window.show()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    app = FrozenBinaryApp('FrozenApp', 'org.example.frozenapp')
    app.main_loop()

```

TITLE: Contributing to Toga's GTK4 Update
DESCRIPTION: Details on how to contribute to updating Toga's GTK backend from GTK3 to GTK4. This involves updating widget APIs and handling potential API differences.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/how-to/contribute/code.rst#_snippet_16

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Contribute to the GTK4 update

Update Toga's GTK backend from GTK3 to GTK4.

Process:
1. Enable GTK4 support by setting the `TOGA_GTK=4` environment variable.
2. Select a widget with GTK3 support and update its API to support GTK4.
3. Identify unported widgets by checking the GTK probe for an "if GTK4, skip" block.
4. Implement conditional branches for significant API differences between GTK3 and GTK4.

Example:
Refer to the Label widget for examples of GTK3/GTK4 conditional implementation.
```

TITLE: Toga Cascading Window Positioning
DESCRIPTION: Implements cascading positioning for newly created windows, ensuring each window appears offset from the previous one.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_48

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

class CascadingWindowApp(toga.App):
    def startup(self):
        self.main_window = toga.MainWindow(title=self.formal_name)
        self.main_window.show()

        # Create additional windows with cascading positions
        for i in range(3):
            win = toga.Window(title=f'Window {i+1}')
            win.show()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    app = CascadingWindowApp('CascadeApp', 'org.example.cascadeapp')
    app.main_loop()

```

TITLE: Toga GTK Backend Dependency: PyGObject Pinning
DESCRIPTION: For users running on Ubuntu 22.04, Debian 11, or Debian 12, a manual pin for `PyGObject==3.50.0` is required. Recent PyGObject releases depend on `girepository-2.0`, which is not available on older Debian-based systems. This manual pinning is necessary because Python's packaging system cannot express dependencies on system packages.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_37

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
APIDOC:
  GTK Backend Dependency Management: PyGObject Pinning

Description:
  Users on specific Debian/Ubuntu versions require manual pinning of PyGObject due to system dependency mismatches.

Details:
  - Target Systems: Ubuntu 22.04, Debian 11, Debian 12.
  - Required Pin: `PyGObject==3.50.0`.
  - Reason: Newer PyGObject versions require `girepository-2.0`, absent in older Debian-based systems.
  - Workaround: Manual pinning is necessary as Python packaging cannot declare system package dependencies.

Instructions:
  Add `PyGObject==3.50.0` to your project's requirements file (e.g., `requirements.txt`).
```

TITLE: Toga OptionContainer Content Management API Changes
DESCRIPTION: The `OptionContainer.add()`, `remove()`, and `insert()` methods have been removed to resolve ambiguity with base widget methods. Developers should now use `OptionContainer.content.append()`, `OptionContainer.content.remove()`, and `OptionContainer.content.insert()` for managing tab content.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/background/project/releases.rst#_snippet_33

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
OptionContainer:
  Removed methods:
    add()
    remove()
    insert()
  New methods for content management:
    content.append(widget)
    content.remove(widget)
    content.insert(index, widget)
```

TITLE: Toga API Events Design
DESCRIPTION: Outlines Toga's event handling mechanism, including naming conventions, handler signatures, and behavior when properties change programmatically.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/how-to/topics/api-design.rst#_snippet_3

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Events:
  - Purpose: Notify app of user actions
  - Assignment: Assign regular or async callable to event handler property
  - Naming: Always begin with 'on_' (e.g., on_press, not on_click)
  - Handler signature:
    - First argument: The widget itself
    - Other arguments: Event-specific information as keyword arguments
    - Forward compatibility: Handlers should declare '**kwargs'
  - Property change events:
    - New value visible within handler
    - Setting property programmatically generates event (unless value is existing)
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Shorthand for toga.Row and toga.Column
DESCRIPTION: Introduces `toga.Row` and `toga.Column` as convenient shorthands for `toga.Box(style=Pack(direction=...))`. This simplifies the creation of common layout structures.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_17

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from toga import Box, Row, Column, Label

# Instead of:
# row_box = Box(direction='row', children=[Label('A'), Label('B')])

# You can now use:
row_widget = Row(children=[Label('A'), Label('B')])
column_widget = Column(children=[Label('C'), Label('D')])
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Modify WebView API for DOM Access, JavaScript Evaluation, and Input
DESCRIPTION: The 'get_dom()' method has been removed from 'WebView'. 'evaluate_javascript()' now supports both synchronous (with 'on_result' callback) and asynchronous (awaitable) contexts. The 'on_key_down' handler is removed, requiring embedded JavaScript or 'Command' for key input. 'invoke_javascript()' is removed, replaced by 'evaluate_javascript()'. Additionally, local 'file://' URLs are now explicitly prohibited.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/background/project/releases.rst#_snippet_26

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
WebView:
  Method 'get_dom()': removed.
  Method 'evaluate_javascript()':
    Behavior: now works in synchronous (with on_result callback) and asynchronous (awaitable) contexts.
  Handler 'on_key_down': removed.
    Alternative: use embedded JavaScript handler or Command with key shortcut.
  Method 'invoke_javascript()': removed. Replaced by 'evaluate_javascript()'.
  Local 'file://' URLs: explicitly prohibited.
```

TITLE: Rename toga.App Full Screen APIs to Presentation Mode
DESCRIPTION: Documents the renaming of `toga.App.enter_full_screen` and `toga.App.exit_full_screen` to `toga.App.enter_presentation_mode` and `toga.App.exit_presentation_mode`, respectively. This change aims to clarify terminology and avoid ambiguity with window full screen.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/background/project/releases.rst#_snippet_2

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
toga.App:
  enter_full_screen(): Deprecated. Use enter_presentation_mode().
  exit_full_screen(): Deprecated. Use exit_presentation_mode().
  enter_presentation_mode(): Enters presentation mode for the app.
  exit_presentation_mode(): Exits presentation mode for the app.
```

```
import toga

class FlexibleLifecycleApp(toga.App):
    def startup(self):
        # App can start and manage its own windows or background tasks
        self.window1 = toga.Window(title='Window 1')
        self.window1.show()

if __name__ == '__main__':

TITLE: API Changes for Window.as_image() and Canvas.as_image()
DESCRIPTION: The macOS implementations of `Window.as_image()` and `Canvas.as_image()` now return images in native device resolution instead of CSS pixel resolution. This results in higher-quality images on Retina displays, effectively doubling their size.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_110

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Backward Incompatible Changes:

macOS Window.as_image() and Canvas.as_image() API Changes:
  - The macOS implementations of `Window.as_image()` and `Canvas.as_image()` APIs now return images in native device resolution, not CSS pixel resolution.
  - This change will result in images that are double the previous size on Retina displays.
  - Related issue: #1930
```

TITLE: Registering Custom Fonts
DESCRIPTION: Explains how to register custom font files with Toga, making them available for use in applications. It covers registering fonts from separate files and single files containing multiple weights.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/resources/fonts.rst#_snippet_1

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

# Register the user font with name "Roboto"
toga.Font.register("Roboto", "resources/Roboto-Regular.ttf")

# Create a label with the new font.
my_label = toga.Label("Hello World", font_family="Roboto")
```

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga
from toga.style.pack import BOLD

# Register a regular and bold font, contained in separate font files
toga.Font.register("Roboto", "resources/Roboto-Regular.ttf")
toga.Font.register("Roboto", "resources/Roboto-Bold.ttf", weight=BOLD)

# Register a single font file that contains both a regular and bold weight
toga.Font.register("Bahnschrift", "resources/Bahnschrift.ttf")
toga.Font.register("Bahnschrift", "resources/Bahnschrift.ttf", weight=BOLD)
```

TITLE: Toga Window API Restrictions: show()/hide()
DESCRIPTION: The `show()` and `hide()` methods for Toga windows can no longer be invoked when a window is in a `MINIMIZED`, `FULLSCREEN`, or `PRESENTATION` state. This restriction prevents potential inconsistencies in window state management.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_35

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
APIDOC:
  Window State Restrictions: show() and hide()

Description:
  The `show()` and `hide()` methods are restricted when a window is in specific states.

Details:
  - `show()` and `hide()` cannot be called on windows that are `MINIMIZED`.
  - `show()` and `hide()` cannot be called on windows that are `FULLSCREEN`.
  - `show()` and `hide()` cannot be called on windows that are `PRESENTATION`.

Backward Incompatible Changes:
  - Code attempting to call `show()` or `hide()` on windows in these states will now raise an error.
```

TITLE: Toga Command Retrieval by ID
DESCRIPTION: Enables commands to be retrieved by their ID, allowing runtime manipulation of system-installed commands like 'About' or 'Visit Homepage'.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_52

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

class CommandIDApp(toga.App):
    def startup(self):
        self.main_window = toga.MainWindow(title=self.formal_name)
        self.main_window.show()

        # Example: Accessing a system command by ID (e.g., 'about')
        about_command = self.commands.get('about')
        if about_command:
            print("Found 'About' command.")
            # You could potentially modify or disable it here

if __name__ == '__main__':
    app = CommandIDApp('CommandIDApp', 'org.example.commandidapp')
    app.main_loop()

```

TITLE: Toga on_running Event Handler
DESCRIPTION: Adds an `on_running` event handler to `toga.App`, which is triggered when the application's main loop begins execution.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_55

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

class RunningEventApp(toga.App):
    def startup(self):
        self.main_window = toga.MainWindow(title=self.formal_name)
        self.main_window.show()

    def on_running(self):
        print("Toga app's main loop is now running.")

if __name__ == '__main__':
    app = RunningEventApp('RunningEventApp', 'org.example.runningeventapp')
    app.main_loop()

```

TITLE: Location Service Permissions
DESCRIPTION: Explains how to check for and request location service permissions. This includes requesting foreground and background location access, and handling potential PermissionError exceptions.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/hardware/location.rst#_snippet_1

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

# Check if permission is already granted
if toga.location.has_permission():
    print("Permission granted")
else:
    # Request foreground permission
    toga.location.request_permission()

# Request background permission (must be after foreground permission is granted)
toga.location.request_background_permission()

# Check background permission
toga.location.has_background_permission()
```

TITLE: Toga Length Units and CSS Pixel Mapping
DESCRIPTION: Details the CSS units used by Toga's public API, their physical size variations across devices, and platform-specific implementations.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/how-to/topics/layout.rst#_snippet_1

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Toga Length Units (CSS Units):
  - Public API Usage: Toga uses CSS units.
  - CSS Pixel:
      - Desktop: ~1/96 inch (0.26 mm)
      - Phone: ~1/160 inch (0.16 mm)
  - CSS Point:
      - Value: 1.33 CSS pixels.
      - Usage: Only for font sizes.
  - Other Lengths: Expressed as pixels.
  - Platform-Specific Implementations:
      - macOS/iOS: One CSS pixel = one "point" (1, 2, or 3 linear physical pixels).
      - Windows: One CSS pixel = one physical pixel at 100% scale; adjusted for higher scale factors.
      - Android: One CSS pixel = one "dp".
```

TITLE: Toga Widget Layers
DESCRIPTION: Explains the three layers of Toga widgets: Interface, Implementation, and Native. The Interface layer is the public API, the Implementation layer is platform-specific, and the Native layer refers to the system's widget toolkit.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/internals/architecture.rst#_snippet_0

LANGUAGE: text
CODE:
```
Interface Layer:
  - Public, documented API for widgets.
  - Defines high-level design concepts.
  - Responsible for input validation and persistent value storage.
  - Stores style and layout attributes.
  - Defined in `toga-core`.

Implementation Layer:
  - Platform-specific representation of widgets.
  - Named after the toolkit (e.g., `toga-cocoa`, `toga-gtk`).
  - Provides a private, internal API for the interface layer.
  - Not intended for end-user consumption.
  - Corresponds to one interface widget; not all interface widgets have an implementation on all platforms.
  - Obtained via the `_impl` attribute of an interface widget.

Native Layer:
  - Represents widgets required by the system's widget toolkit.
  - Accessed via bridging libraries or Python-native APIs.
  - Usually provided by system-level APIs, not Toga.
  - Most implementation widgets have a single native widget.
  - Can use multiple native widgets for complex functionality, with a primary 'container' widget.
  - Interface widget obtained via `interface` attribute of implementation widget.
  - Implementation widget obtained via `impl` attribute of native widget.
```

TITLE: Toga App Paths
DESCRIPTION: Toga's app path properties now create the path on demand if it doesn't exist and cache the paths upon first retrieval for efficiency. This ensures that necessary directories for app data are available when needed.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_2

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

app = toga.App("My App", "com.example.myapp")

# Accessing a path property will create it if it doesn't exist
config_path = app.paths.config
print(f"Config path: {config_path}")

# Subsequent accesses will use the cached path
cache_path = app.paths.cache
print(f"Cache path: {cache_path}")
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Toga Command and Group Keyword-Only Arguments
DESCRIPTION: The optional arguments for `toga.Command` and `toga.Group` are now keyword-only. This change enforces clearer usage by requiring arguments to be explicitly named, improving code readability and reducing potential errors.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_193

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from toga.command import Command, Group

# Previously possible (but now disallowed):
# cmd = Command('My Command', 'icon.png', 'description', 'group', 'shortcut', False, True)

# Correct usage (keyword-only arguments):
cmd = Command(
    text='My Command',
    icon='icon.png',
    description='description',
    group='group',
    shortcut='shortcut',
    is_group=False,
    enabled=True
)
```

TITLE: SplitContainer with Uneven Split (Flex Values)
DESCRIPTION: Illustrates how to configure an uneven initial split in a SplitContainer using flex values.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/containers/splitcontainer.rst#_snippet_2

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

split = toga.SplitContainer()
left_container = toga.Box()
right_container = toga.ScrollContainer()

split.content = [(left_container, 3), (right_container, 2)]
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Toga OptionContainer API Changes
DESCRIPTION: Increment/decrement of current tab is deprecated. Use `container.current_tab = container.current_tab.index + 1`. `add`, `remove`, `insert` methods are removed; use `content.append`, `content.remove`, `content.insert` instead. `on_select` no longer provides the `option` argument.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_181

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from toga.widgets import OptionContainer

# Incrementing tab:
# container.current_tab += 1  # Deprecated
# Use: container.current_tab = container.current_tab.index + 1

# Managing content:
# container.add(widget)  # Removed
# Use: container.content.append(widget)

# on_select handler:
# def on_tab_select(widget, option):
#     pass # 'option' argument removed
# Use: current_tab property to get selected tab

```

TITLE: macOS WebView Debugging
DESCRIPTION: Enables the content inspector for WebView on macOS versions prior to Ventura by setting a user default.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/widgets/webview.rst#_snippet_1

LANGUAGE: console
CODE:
```
defaults write com.example.appname WebKitDeveloperExtras -bool true

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Create Table with Dictionaries
DESCRIPTION: Illustrates creating a Toga Table widget using a list of dictionaries for data. This method allows for additional data fields not displayed in the table and flexible column ordering.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/widgets/table.rst#_snippet_1

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

table = toga.Table(
    headings=["Name", "Age"],
    data=[
        {"name": "Arthur Dent", "age": 42, "planet": "Earth"},
        {"name": "Ford Prefect", "age": 37, "planet": "Betelgeuse Five"},
        {"name": "Tricia McMillan", "age": 38, "planet": "Earth"},
    ]
)

# Get the details of the first item in the data:
row = table.data[0]
print(f"{row.name}, who is age {row.age}, is from {row.planet}")
```

TITLE: Toga Data Source Listeners
DESCRIPTION: Explains how data sources use a Listener interface to notify registered objects about data changes. Widgets are automatically added as listeners when assigned a data source. Any object can act as a listener.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/topics/data-sources.rst#_snippet_1

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from toga.sources import DataSource, Listener

class MyListener(Listener):
    def notify(self, source, event_type, **kwargs):
        print(f"Data source changed: {event_type}")

# Assuming 'data_source' is an instance of a Toga DataSource
# listener = MyListener()
# data_source.add_listener(listener)

# Widgets automatically become listeners when assigned:
# table = Table(data=my_list_source)
# selection = Selection(items=my_list_source)
```

TITLE: Toga Built-in Data Source API Definitions
DESCRIPTION: API definitions for Toga's core data source classes and related interfaces, including ValueSource, ListSource, TreeSource, Row, Node, Listener, and the base Source class for custom implementations.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/how-to/topics/data-sources.rst#_snippet_0

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
class toga.sources.ValueSource:
  description: Manages a single value.
  attributes:
    value: Any
      The single attribute that holds the value to be rendered.
```

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
class toga.sources.ListSource:
  description: Manages a list of items, each with one or more values.
  behavior:
    - Supports standard list manipulation methods.
    - Returns toga.sources.Row objects for each item.
```

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
class toga.sources.Row:
  description: Represents an item within a ListSource.
  attributes:
    Any
      Attributes of this object are the values to be displayed for the row.
```

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
class toga.sources.TreeSource:
  description: Manages a hierarchy of items, each with one or more values.
  behavior:
    - Behaves like a list.
    - Returns toga.sources.Node objects for each item.
```

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
class toga.sources.Node:
  description: Represents an item within a TreeSource.
  attributes:
    Any
      Attributes of this object are the values to be displayed for the node.
  properties:
    children: list[toga.sources.Node]
      Accessible using the list interface on the Node object.
```

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
interface toga.sources.Listener:
  description: Interface for objects that register to receive notifications from data sources.
  events_notified:
    - Adding a new item
    - Removing an existing item
    - Changing an attribute of an existing item
    - Clearing an entire data source
```

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
class toga.sources.Source:
  description: Base class for custom data source implementations.
  behavior:
    - Implements the notification API for listeners.
```

TITLE: Toga 0.4.7 Release Notes
DESCRIPTION: Release notes for Toga version 0.4.7. This release includes feature enhancements for the GTK backend, utilizing PyGObject's native asyncio handling, and adds support for the ActivityIndicator widget on iOS.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_44

LANGUAGE: text
CODE:
```
0.4.7 (2024-09-18)
==================

Features
--------

* The GTK backend was modified to use PyGObject's native asyncio handling, instead of GBulb. (`#2550 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2550>`__)
* The ActivityIndicator widget is now supported on iOS. (`#2829 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2829>`__)

Bugfixes
--------


```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Toga BaseStyle.apply() Method Signature Change
DESCRIPTION: The `apply` method in `BaseStyle` (and `Pack`) has been updated. It now accepts only the property name, and the style object determines the value internally. Previously, it took both property name and value. User code calling this method with two arguments will receive a `DeprecationWarning`.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_39

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
class BaseStyle:
    def apply(self, property_name: str):
        # ... internal logic to apply style based on property_name and current value
        pass

class Pack(BaseStyle):
    pass

# Example of old usage (will trigger DeprecationWarning):
# style_instance.apply('color', 'red')

# Example of new usage:
# style_instance.apply('color')
```

TITLE: Toga Built-in Data Sources Overview
DESCRIPTION: Describes the three built-in data source types in Toga: ValueSource for single values, ListSource for lists of items, and TreeSource for hierarchical data. These are generally wrappers around Python's primitive collection types.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/topics/data-sources.rst#_snippet_0

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from toga.sources import ValueSource, ListSource, TreeSource, Row, Node

# ValueSource: Manages a single value
# value_source = ValueSource(initial_value='Default Text')

# ListSource: Manages a list of items, each as a Row object
# list_source = ListSource([Row(name='Item 1', value=10), Row(name='Item 2', value=20)])

# TreeSource: Manages hierarchical data, with items as Node objects
# tree_source = TreeSource([Node(name='Root', children=[Node(name='Child 1'), Node(name='Child 2')])])
```

TITLE: Deprecated add_background_task API

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_76

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
# Deprecated usage:
# app.add_background_task(my_coroutine)

# Recommended usage:
# Option 1: Using on_running event handler
# class MyApp(toga.App):
#     def on_running(self):
#         asyncio.create_task(my_coroutine())

# Option 2: Direct asyncio usage
# import asyncio
# asyncio.create_task(my_coroutine())
```

TITLE: WebView Method Removals and Changes in Toga
DESCRIPTION: get_dom() and invoke_javascript() methods have been removed from WebView. on_key_down handler is also removed. file:// URLs are prohibited. Use evaluate_javascript() instead of invoke_javascript().

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_172

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from toga.widgets import WebView

webview = WebView()

# Removed methods:
# webview.get_dom()
# webview.invoke_javascript('some_function()')
# webview.on_key_down = my_handler

# Prohibited URL scheme:
# webview.url = 'file:///path/to/file.html'

# Recommended alternative for invoke_javascript:
webview.evaluate_javascript('some_function()')
```

TITLE: Canvas fill() and stroke() Return Type Change
DESCRIPTION: The 'fill()' and 'stroke()' methods now return simple drawing operations instead of context managers. Attempting to use them as context managers on a context will raise an exception. Using them on a Canvas will raise a warning but return the appropriate context manager.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/background/project/releases.rst#_snippet_42

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Canvas.fill()
  Returns: Simple drawing operation (not context manager)

Canvas.stroke()
  Returns: Simple drawing operation (not context manager)

Usage:
  with context.fill(): # Raises exception if context is not Canvas
  with canvas.fill(): # Returns context manager (with warning)
```

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

# Assuming Textual backend is configured for Toga
# This change primarily affects the underlying integration.

class TextualBackendApp(toga.App):
    def startup(self):
        self.main_window = toga.MainWindow(title=self.formal_name)
        self.main_window.show()

if __name__ == '__main__':

    app.main_loop()

```
