TITLE: Toga API Renaming: Full Screen to Presentation Mode
DESCRIPTION: The 'Full screen mode' API in Toga has been renamed to 'Presentation mode' to avoid ambiguity with window-specific full-screen modes. The methods `toga.App.enter_full_screen` and `toga.App.exit_full_screen` are now `toga.App.enter_presentation_mode` and `toga.App.exit_presentation_mode` respectively. This change aims to provide clearer naming for application-level display states.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_31

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
APIDOC:
  toga.App.enter_full_screen() -> toga.App.enter_presentation_mode()
  toga.App.exit_full_screen() -> toga.App.exit_presentation_mode()

Description:
  Renamed application display modes to avoid ambiguity.

Details:
  - 'Full screen mode' at the app level is now 'Presentation mode'.
  - Corresponding API methods have been updated for clarity.

Backward Incompatible Changes:
  - Users of `enter_full_screen` and `exit_full_screen` must update their code to use the new method names.
```

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Table:
  Renamed event handler:
    on_double_click -> on_activate
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Toga Pack Property to CSS Declaration Equivalents
DESCRIPTION: This section details how specific Toga Pack properties are mapped to CSS declarations. It covers layout direction, display type, flex behavior, font sizing, dimensions, and margins, providing the exact CSS equivalent for each Toga property.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/style/pack.rst#_snippet_15

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Pack Property to CSS Declaration Mapping:

============================= ===================================================
Pack property                 CSS property
============================= ===================================================
"direction: <str>"          "flex-direction: <str>"
"display: pack"             "display: flex"
"flex: <int>"               If "direction = row" and "width" is set,
                                  or "direction = column" and "height" is set,
                                  ignore. Otherwise, "flex: <int> 0 auto".
"font_size: <int>"          "font-size: <int>pt"
"height: <value>"           "height: <value>px" if value is an integer;
                                  "height: auto" if value is "none".
"margin_top: <int>"         "margin-top: <int>px"
"margin_bottom: <int>"      "margin-bottom: <int>px"
"margin_left: <int>"        "margin-left: <int>px"
"margin_right: <int>"       "margin-right: <int>px"
"text_direction: <str>"     "direction: <str>"
"width: <value>"            "width: <value>px" if value is an integer;
                                  "width: auto" if value is "none".
============================= ===================================================

General Rule:
All other Pack declarations should be used as-is as CSS declarations, with underscores being converted to dashes (e.g., "background_color" becomes "background-color").
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Toga MainWindow on_close Handler
DESCRIPTION: Adds an `on_close` handler to `MainWindow`, allowing custom logic to execute before the app exits when the main window is closed.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_53

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

class CloseHandlerApp(toga.App):
    def startup(self):
        self.main_window = toga.MainWindow(title=self.formal_name)
        self.main_window.on_close = self.handle_close
        self.main_window.show()

    def handle_close(self, widget):
        print("Main window close requested. Performing checks...")
        # Add logic here to decide if the window should actually close
        # If you return False, the window will not close.
        return True # Allow the close to proceed

if __name__ == '__main__':
    app = CloseHandlerApp('CloseHandlerApp', 'org.example.closehandlerapp')
    app.main_loop()

```

DESCRIPTION: Enables layout debugging in Toga by rendering widgets with different background colors to visualize space allocation. This can be activated by setting the environment variable TOGA_DEBUG_LAYOUT=1 or directly in code via toga.Widget.DEBUG_LAYOUT_ENABLED = True.

# Or set directly in code
import toga
toga.Widget.DEBUG_LAYOUT_ENABLED = True
```

TITLE: Widget ID Handling
DESCRIPTION: New widgets with an ID that matches a previously used ID no longer cause an error, improving the robustness of widget management.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_116

LANGUAGE: python

TITLE: Toga Asynchronous Callbacks
DESCRIPTION: The use of synchronous 'on_result' callbacks for dialogs and 'Webview.evaluate_javascript()' calls is deprecated. Developers should migrate to the asynchronous versions of these methods for better performance and responsiveness.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_129

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

# Deprecated synchronous callback
# def handle_result(result):
#     print(f"Result: {result}")
# dialog.show_modal(on_result=handle_result)

# Recommended asynchronous callback
async def handle_result_async(result):
    print(f"Result: {result}")

# Assuming 'dialog' is a Toga dialog instance
# await dialog.show_modal_async(on_result=handle_result_async)

# Similarly for Webview.evaluate_javascript
# webview = toga.WebView()
# def handle_js_result(result):
#     print(f"JS Result: {result}")
# webview.evaluate_javascript('1 + 1', on_result=handle_js_result) # Deprecated
# await webview.evaluate_javascript_async('1 + 1', on_result=handle_js_result)

```

TITLE: toga.App.current_window Behavior with Dialogs on macOS
DESCRIPTION: Clarifies the corrected behavior of `toga.App.current_window` on macOS. When a dialog is in focus, this property now accurately returns the host window, resolving a previous `AttributeError`.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/background/project/releases.rst#_snippet_0

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
toga.App:
  current_window:
    Platform: macOS
    Behavior: When a dialog is in focus, returns the host window.
    Previous Behavior: Raised AttributeError.
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Toga macOS Fullscreen Window Content
DESCRIPTION: Ensures macOS apps in fullscreen mode correctly display contents of windows using `toga.Box()` as top-level content.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_65

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

class MacOSFullscreenApp(toga.App):
    def startup(self):
        self.main_window = toga.MainWindow(title=self.formal_name)
        
        # Content using toga.Box should render correctly in fullscreen on macOS
        content = toga.Box(
            children=[
                toga.Label('Fullscreen Content')
            ]
        )
        self.main_window.content = content
        self.main_window.show()
        # self.main_window.full_screen()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    app = MacOSFullscreenApp('MacOSFullscreenApp', 'org.example.macosfullscreenapp')
    app.main_loop()

```

TITLE: WebView Background Fix on Windows
DESCRIPTION: WebViews on Windows no longer exhibit a black background when they are resized. This resolves a visual artifact and ensures a consistent rendering experience.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_161

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
# This is a visual bug fix for the Windows WebView implementation.
# No code changes are required by the user; the rendering will be correct by default.

```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Canvas Drawing Operation Changes
DESCRIPTION: The `fill()` and `stroke()` methods now return simple drawing operations instead of context managers. Using them as context managers will raise an exception. Methods generating new contexts have been renamed: `context()` to `Context()`, `closed_path()` to `ClosedPath()`, `fill()` to `Fill()`, and `stroke()` to `Stroke()`. The `preserve` option on `Fill()` operations is deprecated. Drawing operations like `arc` and `line_to` must now be invoked on the canvas's root context (`canvas.context`), not directly on the canvas.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_188

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
# Old:
# canvas.fill()
# canvas.stroke()
# canvas.context()
# canvas.closed_path()

# New:
# canvas.Fill()
# canvas.Stroke()
# canvas.Context()
# canvas.ClosedPath()

# Old:
# canvas.arc(...)
# canvas.line_to(...)

# New:
# canvas.context.arc(...)
# canvas.context.line_to(...)
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Add, Update, and Remove Map Pins
DESCRIPTION: Illustrates how to add pins to a MapView, including initializing with pins, adding new pins dynamically, updating existing pins' properties (location and title), and removing pins individually or all at once.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/widgets/mapview.rst#_snippet_1

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

mapview = toga.MapView(
    pins=[
        toga.MapPin((-31.95064, 115.85889), title="Yagan Square"),
    ]
)

# Create a new pin, and add it to the map
brutus = toga.MapPin((41.50375, -81.69475), title="Brutus was here")
mapview.pins.add(brutus)

# Update the pin label and position
brutus.location = (40.440831, -79.991162)
brutus.title = "Brutus will be here"

# Remove the Brutus pin
mapview.pins.remove(brutus)

# Remove all pins
mapview.pins.clear()
```

TITLE: Toga Document Class Reference
DESCRIPTION: API documentation for the `toga.Document` class, which serves as the base class for representing files within a Toga application. It outlines the core attributes and methods for managing document content, modification status, and file operations.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/resources/document.rst#_snippet_2

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
autoclass:: toga.Document
  description: A representation of a file on disk that will be displayed in one or more windows.
  attributes:
    description (str): A human-readable description of the document type.
    extensions (list[str]): A list of file extensions associated with this document type.
    main_window (toga.DocumentMainWindow): The primary window associated with this document.
    path (pathlib.Path): The file path of the document.
  methods:
    create(): Creates the main window for the document.
    read(): Reads the content of the document's file and populates the UI.
    write(): Writes the current UI content back to the document's file.
    touch(widget=None, **kwargs): Marks the document as modified.
```

TITLE: WebView JavaScript Evaluation in Toga
DESCRIPTION: The evaluate_javascript() method on WebView now supports both synchronous and asynchronous contexts. Use an on_result callback for synchronous calls or await the result asynchronously.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_171

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from toga.widgets import WebView

webview = WebView()

# Synchronous evaluation with callback
def handle_result(result):
    print(f"JS evaluation result: {result}")

webview.evaluate_javascript('document.title', on_result=handle_result)

# Asynchronous evaluation (using async/await)
# async def evaluate_async():
#     result = await webview.evaluate_javascript('document.body.innerHTML')
#     print(f"Async JS result: {result}")
# asyncio.run(evaluate_async())
```

TITLE: Toga PackMixin for Layout
DESCRIPTION: Provides layout management capabilities for Toga widgets, allowing them to be packed and arranged within their parent containers. This mixin is inherited by the base Widget class.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/widgets/widget.rst#_snippet_1

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
toga.widgets.base.PackMixin:
  Mixin class for layout management.

  Methods:
    pack(self, **style)
      Packs the widget into its parent container with the specified style.
      Parameters:
        **style: Keyword arguments defining the packing style (e.g., 'left', 'right', 'fill', 'expand').

    unpack(self)
      Removes the widget from its parent container.

  Attributes:
    (Attributes related to layout are managed internally or through the pack() method)
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Update macOS Image Resolution for Window.as_image() and Canvas.as_image()
DESCRIPTION: The `as_image()` methods for `Window` and `Canvas` on macOS now return images in native device resolution, not CSS pixel resolution. This backward-incompatible change means images will be double the previous size on Retina displays.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/background/project/releases.rst#_snippet_17

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
API Change:
- Method: Window.as_image()
  Platform: macOS
  Old Behavior: Returns image in CSS pixel resolution.
  New Behavior: Returns image in native device resolution.
  Impact: Images on Retina displays will be double the previous size.

- Method: Canvas.as_image()
  Platform: macOS
  Old Behavior: Returns image in CSS pixel resolution.
  New Behavior: Returns image in native device resolution.
  Impact: Images on Retina displays will be double the previous size.
```

TITLE: Toga 0.3.0 Release Notes
DESCRIPTION: Details the changes in Toga version 0.3.0, including new features and backward incompatible changes. This release introduces layout node size tracking and drops support for Python 3.7.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/travertino/CHANGELOG.rst#_snippet_12

LANGUAGE: release-notes
CODE:
```
0.3.0 (2023-08-16)

Features
--------

* Layout nodes can now track the minimum permitted layout size in addition to the current actual layout size. (`#78 <https://github.com/beeware/travertino/issues/78>`_)


Backward Incompatible Changes
-----------------------------

* Support for Python 3.7 was removed. (`#80 <https://github.com/beeware/travertino/issues/80>`_)


Misc
----


```

DESCRIPTION: Enables the debug layout colors tool by setting the DEBUG_LAYOUT_ENABLED class attribute to True. This allows visual debugging of widget layout by assigning distinct colors to containers.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/topics/debugging.rst#_snippet_0

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

toga.Widget.DEBUG_LAYOUT_ENABLED = True
```

TITLE: Direct Style Property Access
DESCRIPTION: Allows style properties to be passed directly to a widget's constructor or accessed as attributes, eliminating the need for an explicit `style` object in many cases. This streamlines widget styling.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_18

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from toga import Button

# Instead of:
# from toga.style import Pack
# button = Button('Click Me', style=Pack(background_color='#00FF00'))

# You can now use:
button = Button('Click Me', background_color='#00FF00')
```

TITLE: Toga Utility Types
DESCRIPTION: Defines utility data structures and types used by Toga APIs for managing application data and state.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/index.rst#_snippet_9

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from toga import types

# Example usage (conceptual):
# position = types.Point(x=10, y=20)
# size = types.Size(width=100, height=50)
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Toga Font Handling
DESCRIPTION: Toga's font handling has been updated. Previously, unknown font families would silently fall back to a system font. Now, an UnknownFontError is raised, providing clearer feedback when a specified font cannot be loaded. The Pack widget's font_family property now accepts a list of font names, using the first available font.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_1

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from toga.style import Pack
from toga.fonts import UnknownFontError

# Example of setting font family with a list
# The first available font in the list will be used.
widget_style = Pack(font_family=['Helvetica Neue', 'Arial', 'sans-serif'])

# Example of catching the error when a font is not found
try:
    # Attempt to load a font that doesn't exist
    font = toga.Font('NonExistentFont', 12)
except UnknownFontError as e:
    print(f"Error loading font: {e}")
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Platform-Specific Permissions (iOS/macOS/Android)
DESCRIPTION: Outlines the specific permission keys and configurations required in the application's manifest or property list files for iOS, macOS, and Android to enable location services.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/hardware/location.rst#_snippet_4

LANGUAGE: text
CODE:
```
Platform Permissions:

- iOS:
  - NSLocationWhenInUseUsageDescription (Info.plist)
  - NSLocationAlwaysAndWhenInUseUsageDescription (for background tracking)
  - UIBackgroundModes (location, processing) (Info.plist for background tracking)

- macOS:
  - com.apple.security.personal-information.location entitlement
  - NSLocationUsageDescription (Info.plist)

- Android:
  - android.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Pack Style Properties
DESCRIPTION: Defines the various style properties used by the Pack layout engine in Toga, including their accepted values, initial states, and behavior.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/style/pack.rst#_snippet_0

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
display:
  Value: "pack" or "none"
  Initial value: "pack"
  Description: Used to define how to display the widget. A value of "pack" will apply the pack layout algorithm to this node and its descendants. A value of "none" removes the widget from the layout entirely. Space will be allocated for the widget as if it were there, but the widget itself will not be visible.

visibility:
  Value: "hidden" or "visible"
  Initial value: "visible"
  Description: Used to define whether the widget should be drawn. A value of "visible" means the widget will be displayed. A value of "hidden" removes the widget from view, but allocates space for the widget as if it were still in the layout. Any children of a hidden widget are implicitly removed from view. If a previously hidden widget is made visible, any children of the widget with a visibility of "hidden" will remain hidden. Any descendants of the hidden child will also remain hidden, regardless of their visibility.

direction:
  Value: "row" or "column"
  Initial value: "row"
  Description: The packing direction for children of the box. A value of "column" indicates children will be stacked vertically, from top to bottom. A value of "row" indicates children will be packed horizontally; left-to-right if text_direction is "ltr", or right-to-left if text_direction is "rtl".

align_items:
  Value: "start", "center", or "end"
  Initial value: "start"
  Aliases: vertical_align_items in a row, horizontal_align_items in a column
  Description: The alignment of this box's children along the cross axis. A row's cross axis is vertical, so "start" aligns children to the top, while "end" aligns them to the bottom. For columns, "start" is on the left if text_direction is "ltr", and the right if rtl.

justify_content:
  Value: "start", "center", or "end"
  Initial value: "start"
  Aliases: horizontal_align_content in a row, vertical_align_content in a column
  Description: The alignment of this box's children along the main axis. A column's main axis is vertical, so "start" aligns children to the top, while "end" aligns them to the bottom. For rows, "start" is on the left if text_direction is "ltr", and the right if "rtl". This property only has an effect if there is some free space in the main axis. For example, if any children have a non-zero flex value, then they will consume all the available space, and justify_content will make no difference to the layout.

gap:
  Value: an integer
  Initial value: 0
  Description: The amount of space to allocate between adjacent children, in CSS pixels.

width:
  Value: an integer or "none"
  Initial value: "none"
  Description: Specify a fixed width for the box, in CSS pixels. The final width for the box may be larger, if the children of the box cannot fit inside the specified space.

height:
  Value: an integer or "none"
  Initial value: "none"
  Description: Specify a fixed height for the box, in CSS pixels. The final height for the box may be larger, if the children of the box cannot fit inside the specified space.

flex:
  Value: a floating-point number
  Initial value: 0.0
  Description: A weighting that is used to compare this box with its siblings when distributing space.
```

TITLE: Toga OptionContainer Enhancements
DESCRIPTION: The OptionContainer widget now supports constructing content using toga.OptionItem objects and has gained an OptionContainer widget for iOS. When inserting or appending tabs, the 'enabled' argument must be a keyword argument, and the first argument is now 'text_or_item'.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_128

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

# Example using OptionItem
option_container = toga.OptionContainer()
option_container.add(
    toga.OptionItem(
        text='Tab 1',
        content=toga.Box()
    )
)

# Example of keyword argument for enabled (backward incompatible change)
# option_container.add(
#     toga.OptionItem(
#         text_or_item='Tab 2',
#         content=toga.Box(),
#         enabled=True  # Must be a keyword argument
#     )
# )

```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Toga Document Handling API
DESCRIPTION: Finalizes the API for Documents and document types, allowing document handling behavior to be defined within the `toga.App` definition.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_49

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

class DocumentApp(toga.App):
    def startup(self):
        self.main_window = toga.MainWindow(title=self.formal_name)
        self.main_window.show()

    # Define document types and their handlers
    def get_document_types(self):
        return [
            toga.DocumentType(roles=['editor'], mime_types=['text/plain'], file_extensions=['.txt'])
        ]

if __name__ == '__main__':
    app = DocumentApp('DocApp', 'org.example.docapp')
    app.main_loop()

```

TITLE: Toga Namespace Changes

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_197

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
# Previously accessible:
# from toga import GROUP_BREAK, SECTION_BREAK, CommandSet

# Now accessible:
from toga.command import GROUP_BREAK, SECTION_BREAK, CommandSet
```

TITLE: Toga App Background Persistence

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

class BackgroundApp(toga.App):
    def startup(self):
        # App logic that can run without a main window
        pass

    def on_exit(self):
        # Cleanup logic when the app exits
        pass

if __name__ == '__main__':
    app = BackgroundApp('MyApp', 'org.example.myapp')
    app.main_loop()

```

TITLE: Toga iOS Navigation Bar Size Handling
DESCRIPTION: Ensures iOS apps correctly account for the navigation bar's size when laying out application content.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_56

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

class iOSLayoutApp(toga.App):
    def startup(self):
        self.main_window = toga.MainWindow(title=self.formal_name)
        
        # Content layout will now correctly consider the navigation bar height
        content = toga.Box(
            children=[
                toga.Label('Content area'),
            ]
        )
        self.main_window.content = content
        self.main_window.show()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    app = iOSLayoutApp('iOSLayoutApp', 'org.example.ioslayoutapp')
    app.main_loop()

```

TITLE: Update TreeSource insert and append for consistency
DESCRIPTION: The 'parent' argument has been removed from 'insert' and 'append' calls on 'TreeSource' to improve consistency with the list API. To insert or append a row into a descendant of a TreeSource root, use 'insert' and 'append' directly on the parent node.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/background/project/releases.rst#_snippet_38

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
TreeSource.insert(parent, index, ...)
  OLD: source.insert(parent, index, ...)
  NEW: parent.insert(index, ...)

TreeSource.insert(None, index, ...)
  OLD: source.insert(None, index, ...)
  NEW: source.insert(index, ...)
```

TITLE: Canvas Event and Path Renaming

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_189

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:

# New:
# canvas.on_activate(handler=my_handler)

# Old:
# canvas.new_path()

# New:
# canvas.begin_path()
```

TITLE: DetailedList Data Order and Event Handling Changes
DESCRIPTION: When constructing a DetailedList from tuples or lists, the order of values has changed from (icon, title, subtitle) to (title, subtitle, icon). The `on_select` handler no longer receives the selected row. The `on_delete` event handler is renamed to `on_primary_action` and is now solely a notification for a 'swipe left' event, requiring the application to handle data deletion.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_187

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
# Old order: (icon, title, subtitle)
# New order: (title, subtitle, icon)

# Old: DetailedList(on_select=my_handler)
# New: DetailedList(on_select=my_handler) # handler no longer receives selected row

# Old: on_delete
# New: on_primary_action
```

TITLE: Toga Dialogs Relative to App
DESCRIPTION: Enables displaying dialogs relative to an application, in addition to being modal to a specific window.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_54

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

class DialogApp(toga.App):
    def startup(self):
        self.main_window = toga.MainWindow(title=self.formal_name)
        self.main_window.show()

        # Show a dialog relative to the app
        dialog = toga.Alert(
            title='App Dialog',
            message='This dialog is relative to the app.',
            primary='OK'
        )
        dialog.show()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    app = DialogApp('DialogApp', 'org.example.dialogapp')
    app.main_loop()

```

TITLE: Toga SplitContainer API Changes
DESCRIPTION: Toga's `SplitContainer` widget no longer supports more than two panels of content. Additionally, the 3-tuple format for specifying `SplitContainer` items, previously used to prevent panel resizing, has been removed. Developers must adapt their `SplitContainer` configurations to these new constraints.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/background/project/releases.rst#_snippet_31

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
toga.SplitContainer:
  - Supports a maximum of 2 content panels.
  - Removed support for 3-tuple item specification for resizing control.
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Travertino Pack Dataclass
DESCRIPTION: The `Pack` style representation in Travertino is now a dataclass. This improves IDE support for property name and type inference when creating `Pack` instances.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_26

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass
class Pack:
    padding: int = 0
    margin: int = 0
    # ... other properties
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Create Tree with List of Tuples Data
DESCRIPTION: Illustrates creating a Toga Tree widget using a list of 2-tuples, where dictionaries provide the data values. This method allows for storing additional data not displayed in the tree and controlling column display order independently. It also shows accessing node data.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/widgets/tree.rst#_snippet_1

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

tree = toga.Tree(
    headings=["Name", "Age"],
    data=[
        (
            {"name": "Earth"},
            [({"name": "Arthur Dent", "age": 42, "status": "Anxious"}, None)]
        ),
        (
            {"name": "Betelgeuse Five"},
            [
                ({"name": "Ford Prefect", "age": 37, "status": "Hoopy"}, None),
                ({"name": "Zaphod Beeblebrox", "age": 47, "status": "Oblivious"}, None),
            ]
        ),
    ]
)

# Get the details of the first child of the second root node:
node = tree.data[1][0]
print(f"{node.name}, who is age {node.age}, is {node.status}")
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Font Argument Changes in Toga
DESCRIPTION: The 'weight', 'style', and 'variant' arguments for Font and Font.register are now keyword-only. This change enforces clearer usage of these parameters.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_168

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from toga.fonts import Font

# Previous usage (may still work but is deprecated)
# font = Font(family='Arial', weight='bold', style='italic', variant='small-caps')

# New keyword-only usage
font = Font(family='Arial', weight='bold', style='italic', variant='small-caps')
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Default Toga Window Title Changed to App Name
DESCRIPTION: The default title of a `toga.Window` is now automatically set to the name of the application, rather than the generic 'Toga'. This provides a more consistent and intuitive user experience by default, reflecting the application's identity in its window title.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/background/project/releases.rst#_snippet_15

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
toga.Window:
  title: str
    Default: The name of the application (previously "Toga").
    Description: The title displayed in the window's title bar.
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Toga Color Format Conversion Caching
DESCRIPTION: Due to the read-only nature of color objects, format conversions (e.g., RGB to HSL and vice-versa) can now cache their results. This means the conversion calculation is performed only once, improving performance for repeated conversions.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/changes/3611.removal.3.rst#_snippet_1

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from toga.colors import rgb, hsl

color_rgb = rgb(1, 0, 0)

# First conversion: calculation is performed
color_hsl_1 = color_rgb.hsl

# Second conversion: result is cached and returned directly
color_hsl_2 = color_rgb.hsl

# Converting to the same type returns the original object
color_rgb_self = color_rgb.rgb

# assert color_hsl_1 is not color_hsl_2 # This assertion might fail if caching is aggressive
# assert color_rgb is color_rgb_self
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Create Tree with Custom Accessors
DESCRIPTION: Shows how to customize data attribute mapping in a Toga Tree by providing an 'accessors' argument. This allows specifying which internal attributes correspond to the displayed column headers, as demonstrated with 'character' for the 'Name' column.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/widgets/tree.rst#_snippet_2

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

tree = toga.Tree(
    headings=["Name", "Age"],
    accessors={"Name", 'character'},
    data=[
        (
            {"character": "Earth"},
            [({"character": "Arthur Dent", "age": 42, "status": "Anxious"}, None)]
        ),
        (
            {"character": "Betelgeuse Five"},
            [
                ({"character": "Ford Prefect", "age": 37, "status": "Hoopy"}, None),
                ({"character": "Zaphod Beeblebrox", "age": 47, "status": "Oblivious"}, None),
            ]
        ),
    ]
)

# Get the details of the first child of the second root node:
node = tree.data[1][0]
print(f"{node.character}, who is age {node.age}, is {node.status}")
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Significant API Changes for Toga Documents and Document-based Apps
DESCRIPTION: The API for Documents and Document-based applications has been significantly modified, requiring existing apps to be updated. The `DocumentApp` base class is no longer required; apps can subclass `App` directly, passing document types as a list of `Document` classes. The `Document` subclass API has also changed, affecting constructor arguments, document type specification, extension definition, and how document modification and closing are handled.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/background/project/releases.rst#_snippet_13

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
toga.App:
  __init__(app_name: str, app_id: str, document_types: list[toga.Document] = None, ...):
    Description: Initialize app with document types.
    Parameters:
      document_types: A list of Document classes that this app can handle.
    Note: toga.DocumentApp base class is no longer required; subclass toga.App directly.

toga.Document:
  __init__(path: str = None, app: toga.App = None):
    Description: Constructor no longer requires a path argument.
    Parameters:
      path: Optional path to the document file.
      app: The application instance.
  description: str (class property)
    Description: Specifies the human-readable document type (replaces previous document_type concept).
  extensions: list[str] (class property)
    Description: Defines allowed file extensions for the document, e.g., ['.txt', '.log'].
  can_close():
    Status: No longer honored.
    Replacement: Document now tracks modification status via touch() and save().
  on_close():
    Description: Default handler uses the document's modification status to control whether it can close.
  touch():
    Description: Marks a document as modified. This flag is cleared upon saving the document.
```

TITLE: Toga Document Path Renaming

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_194

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:

from toga.documents import Document

print(doc.path)

3. Replace all whitespace with ``_``

```

import toga

class GtkAboutDialogApp(toga.App):
    def startup(self):
        self.main_window = toga.MainWindow(title=self.formal_name)
        self.main_window.show()

            app_name=self.formal_name,
            version=self.version,
            developer='Your Name'

if __name__ == '__main__':

TITLE: Create Table with Custom Accessors
DESCRIPTION: Shows how to specify custom accessors for a Toga Table, allowing internal data attributes to differ from the displayed column headings. This provides more control over data mapping.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/widgets/table.rst#_snippet_2

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

table = toga.Table(
    headings=["Name", "Age"],
    accessors={"Name": 'character', "Age": 'age'},
    data=[
        {"character": "Arthur Dent", "age": 42, "planet": "Earth"},
        {"character": "Ford Prefect", "age": 37, "planet": "Betelgeuse Five"},
        {"name": "Tricia McMillan", "age": 38, "planet": "Earth"},
    ]
)

# Get the details of the first item in the data:
row = table.data[0]
print(f"{row.character}, who is age {row.age}, is from {row.planet}")
```

  Deprecated method:

  Recommended replacements:
    append_column(heading, accessor, width=None, min_width=None, alignment=LEFT_ALIGNMENT, factory=None)
    insert_column(index, heading, accessor, width=None, min_width=None, alignment=LEFT_ALIGNMENT, factory=None)

TITLE: Color Class Aliases in Travertino
DESCRIPTION: Explains the renaming and aliasing of color classes in Travertino to better align with CSS standards. Previously, 'rgba' and 'hsla' were distinct from 'rgb' and 'hsl' subclasses. Now, 'rgb' and 'hsl' are preferred, with 'rgba' and 'hsla' serving as direct aliases, preserving alpha channel information.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/changes/3611.removal.1.rst#_snippet_0

LANGUAGE: Python
CODE:
```
from travertino.color import rgb, hsl

# Preferred forms
color1 = rgb(255, 0, 0)
color2 = hsl(120, 1, 0.5)

# Aliases that preserve alpha
color3 = rgb(0, 0, 255, 0.5)
color4 = hsl(240, 1, 0.5, 0.7)

# rgba and hsla are now aliases for rgb and hsl respectively
rgba_color = rgb(255, 255, 255, 0.5)
print(rgba_color.alpha)
# Expected output: 0.5
```

from toga.types import Size, Position

# Example: Getting size and position
# widget_size: Size = widget.size
# widget_position: Position = widget.position

# new_size = widget_size + Size(10, 5)
# new_position = widget_position - Position(5, 0)

# Example: Setting size and position using tuples
# widget.size = (100, 50)
# widget.position = (20, 30)
```

TITLE: Canvas Transformations and Context Manipulation
DESCRIPTION: Allows transformations to be applied to any canvas context, not just the root. The Canvas widget now offers more list-like methods for managing drawing objects within a context.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_149

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from toga.widgets.canvas import Canvas, Context

# Example of applying transformations to a specific context
canvas = Canvas()
context1 = canvas.new_context()
context2 = canvas.new_context()

with context1.transform(scale=2):
    context1.fill_style = 'red'
    context1.fill_rect(10, 10, 50, 50)

with context2.rotate(45):
    context2.fill_style = 'blue'
    context2.fill_rect(100, 100, 50, 50)

# Example of list-like methods for drawing objects
# Assuming 'drawing_objects' is a list of objects in the context
# context1.append(my_shape)
# context1.remove(another_shape)

```

TITLE: Widget Name Changes in Toga
DESCRIPTION: DatePicker has been renamed to DateInput, and TimePicker has been renamed to TimeInput for better consistency across the Toga toolkit.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_173

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from toga.widgets import DateInput, TimeInput

# Previous usage (DatePicker, TimePicker)
# date_picker = DatePicker()
# time_picker = TimePicker()

# New usage
date_input = DateInput()
time_input = TimeInput()
```

TITLE: DetailedList Row Deletion Event Renamed and Behavior Changed
DESCRIPTION: The 'on_delete' event handler for DetailedList has been renamed to 'on_primary_action'. It now serves solely as a notification that a 'swipe left' event (or platform equivalent) has been confirmed. The application is now responsible for performing the actual data deletion.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/background/project/releases.rst#_snippet_41

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
DetailedList Event Handler:
  OLD: on_delete
  NEW: on_primary_action

Behavior:
  OLD: Inconsistent deletion across platforms; some updated data source, some notified.
  NEW: Always a notification; application must handle data deletion.
```

TITLE: Towncrier Change Note Fragment Types
DESCRIPTION: Lists the allowed fragment types for towncrier change notes in the Toga project. Each type (feature, bugfix, doc, removal, misc) is described with its purpose in categorizing changes for release notes.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/how-to/contribute/code.rst#_snippet_53

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Towncrier Fragment Types:

- feature: The PR adds a new behavior or capability.
- bugfix: The PR fixes a bug in the existing implementation.
- doc: The PR is an significant improvement to documentation.
- removal: The PR represents a backwards incompatible change in the Toga API.
- misc: A minor or administrative change that doesn't need to be announced in the release notes.
```

TITLE: Toga ImageConverter Protocol
DESCRIPTION: The ImageConverter protocol defines the interface for classes that convert custom image types to and from Toga's internal Image class. It specifies the methods required for image conversion.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/plugins/image_formats.rst#_snippet_1

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from typing import Any

class ImageConverter:
    def to_toga_image(self, img: Any) -> 'toga.Image':
        """Convert an external image object to a Toga Image object."""
        ...

    def from_toga_image(self, img: 'toga.Image') -> Any:
        """Convert a Toga Image object to an external image object."""
        ...
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Detect Dark Mode Preference (GTK)
DESCRIPTION: This snippet demonstrates how to check if the user has a preference for dark mode using Toga's GTK backend. It leverages GTK's `Gtk.Settings.get_default().props.gtk_application_prefer_dark_theme` property. This is useful for theming applications to match the user's system-wide appearance settings.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/changes/2841.feature.rst#_snippet_0

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga
from toga.style import Pack

class MyApp(toga.App):
    def startup(self):
        # Check if dark mode is preferred
        if self.main_window.app.native.app.settings.gtk_application_prefer_dark_theme:
            print("User prefers dark mode.")
            # Apply dark theme styles or widgets
        else:
            print("User prefers light mode.")
            # Apply light theme styles or widgets

        self.main_window = toga.MainWindow(title=self.formal_name)
        self.main_window.content = toga.Box(children=[
            toga.Label('Hello, Toga!', style=Pack(padding=20))
        ])
        self.main_window.show()

def main():
    return MyApp()

```

TITLE: Deprecate Generator-Based Event Handlers in Toga
DESCRIPTION: Explains the deprecation of generator-based event handlers and provides guidance on converting them to asynchronous co-routines. This involves changing the handler to `async def` and replacing `yield t` with `await asyncio.sleep(t)`.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/background/project/releases.rst#_snippet_3

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Event Handlers:
  Deprecated: Generator-based event handlers.
  Recommended: Asynchronous co-routines.
  Conversion:
    - Change handler to `async def`.
    - Replace `yield t` with `await asyncio.sleep(t)`.
```

LANGUAGE: APIDOC

  Handler signature change:
    Old: on_select(option_container, option)
    New: on_select(option_container)
  To get selected tab: Use option_container.current_tab
```

TITLE: Toga Style Engine and Layout
DESCRIPTION: Toga uses a style engine, such as the built-in Pack engine, to manage widget layout. Each widget has a style object that dictates its positioning. Widgets report an 'intrinsic' size (width and height), which can be fixed or have a minimum. Changes to widget properties require rehinting to recalculate intrinsic size and invalidate layout.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/topics/layout.rst#_snippet_0

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
class Widget:
    def __init__(self, style=None):
        self.style = style or Pack()
        # ... other widget properties

    def rehint(self):
        # Recalculates intrinsic size and invalidates layout
        pass

class Container(Widget):
    def __init__(self, content=None, style=None):
        super().__init__(style)
        self.content = content
        # ... other container properties

    def refresh(self):
        # Refreshes self and any sub-containers
        pass

# Example usage:
# from toga.style.pack import Pack
# button_style = Pack(padding=5)
# my_button = Button('Click Me', style=button_style)
# container_widget.add(my_button)

```

TITLE: WinForms Event Loop Decoupling
DESCRIPTION: The WinForms event loop has been decoupled from the main form. This allows background tasks to execute even when a main window is not present, improving application responsiveness and background processing capabilities.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_156

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:

# This is an internal implementation detail of the WinForms backend.
# No direct user code is typically needed to leverage this change.
# The effect is that background threads or tasks can run more reliably.

```

TITLE: Drawing Operations Must Be Invoked on Canvas Context
DESCRIPTION: Drawing operations (e.g., 'arc', 'line_to') can no longer be invoked directly on a Canvas. Instead, they must be invoked on the root context of the canvas, retrieved via the 'canvas.context' property. Context-creating operations ('Fill', 'Stroke', 'ClosedPath') are not affected.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/background/project/releases.rst#_snippet_48

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Drawing Operations:
  OLD: canvas.arc(...)
  OLD: canvas.line_to(...)
  NEW: canvas.context.arc(...)
  NEW: canvas.context.line_to(...)
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Fill() preserve Option Deprecated
DESCRIPTION: The 'preserve' option on 'Fill()' operations has been deprecated. It was required for an internal optimization and can be safely removed without impact.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/background/project/releases.rst#_snippet_47

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Fill() Option:
  Deprecated: preserve
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Toga Layout Optimization: No Trigger on Unchanged Resize
DESCRIPTION: Toga no longer triggers a window content layout if a resize request does not result in a change in window size. This optimization prevents unnecessary layout calculations and improves performance.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_36

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
APIDOC:
  Layout Optimization: Resize Trigger

Description:
  Layout of window content is no longer triggered if a resize request does not alter the window size.

Details:
  - This change optimizes performance by avoiding redundant layout passes.

Backward Incompatible Changes:
  - No direct backward incompatibility, but behavior regarding resize events has been refined.
```

LANGUAGE: python

# Example:
font1 = Font(family='Arial', size=12)
non_font_object = "Arial"

# This will now raise a TypeError instead of returning False:
# if font1 == non_font_object:
```

TITLE: Toga 0.4.8 Release Notes and Backward Incompatible Changes
DESCRIPTION: Release notes for Toga version 0.4.8. This release includes bug fixes for macOS document selection dialogs and Android event loop integration, and introduces backward incompatible changes by dropping support for Python 3.8 and requiring updates to Android Gradle dependencies.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_43

LANGUAGE: text
CODE:
```
0.4.8 (2024-10-16)
==================

Bugfixes
--------

* On macOS, apps that specify both `document_types` and a `main_window` no longer display the document selection dialog on startup. (`#2860 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2860>`__)
* The integration with Android's event loop has been updated to support Python 3.13. (`#2907 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2907>`__)

Backward Incompatible Changes
-----------------------------

* Toga no longer supports Python 3.8. (`#2888 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2888>`__)
* Android applications should update their Gradle requirements to use version 1.12.0 of the Material library (``com.google.android.material:material:1.12.0``). (`#2890 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2890>`__)
* Android applications should update their Gradle requirements to use version 6.1.20 of the OSMDroid library (``org.osmdroid:osmdroid-android:6.1.20``). (`#2890 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2890>`__)

Misc
----

* `#2868 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2868>`__, `#2869 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2869>`__, `#2870 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2870>`__, `#2876 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2876>`__, `#2877 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2877>`__, `#2884 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2884>`__, `#2885 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2885>`__, `#2886 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2886>`__, `#2887 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2887>`__, `#2893 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2893>`__, `#2897 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2897>`__, `#2898 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2898>`__, `#2899 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2899>`__, `#2900 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2900>`__, `#2901 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2901>`__, `#2902 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2902>`__, `#2903 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2903>`__, `#2904 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2904>`__, `#2905 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2905>`__, `#2906 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2906>`__, `#2912 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2912>`__
```

LANGUAGE: gradle
CODE:
```
com.google.android.material:material:1.12.0
org.osmdroid:osmdroid-android:6.1.20
```

TITLE: Widget Event Handler Return Value
DESCRIPTION: In the Toga UI toolkit, event handlers attached to widgets are now designed to return `None` by default if no explicit return value is provided. This change standardizes the behavior across all widget event handlers, making it clearer what to expect when an event is triggered and no specific action is taken by the handler.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/changes/3582.bugfix.rst#_snippet_0

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
def on_button_press(widget):
    # No explicit return statement here
    pass

# The return value of on_button_press will be None
```

TITLE: Modifying Drawing Objects and Redrawing in Toga Canvas
DESCRIPTION: Shows how to create drawing objects (circle and rectangle) using fill contexts, modify their properties, and request a redraw. It also demonstrates changing the fill color and removing a drawing object.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/widgets/canvas.rst#_snippet_2

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

canvas = toga.Canvas()
with canvas.context.Fill(color="red") as fill:
    circle = fill.arc(x=50, y=50, radius=15)
    rect = fill.rect(x=50, y=50, width=15, height=15)

# Modify properties and redraw
circle.x = 25
circle.y = 25
circle.radius = 5
canvas.redraw()

# Change fill color and redraw
fill.color = "blue"
canvas.redraw()

# Remove an object
fill.remove(rect)
```

TITLE: Define App Exit Handler by Overriding on_exit Method in Toga
DESCRIPTION: The `on_exit` handler for a Toga application can now be defined by overriding the method directly on the `toga.App` subclass. This provides a more direct and object-oriented way to manage application shutdown logic and resources.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/background/project/releases.rst#_snippet_10

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
toga.App:
  on_exit():
    Description: Handler for app exit.
    Usage: Override this method in a toga.App subclass to define custom exit behavior.
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Toga Input Widget Property Renames
DESCRIPTION: Several input widgets in Toga have had their value range properties renamed for consistency. `TimePicker.min_time`/`max_time` are now `TimeInput.min`/`max`. `DatePicker.min_date`/`max_date` are now `DateInput.min`/`max`. `NumberInput.min_value`/`max_value` are now `NumberInput.min`/`max`. `Slider.range` has been replaced by `Slider.min` and `Slider.max`.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/background/project/releases.rst#_snippet_35

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
TimePicker (now TimeInput):
  - min_time -> TimeInput.min
  - max_time -> TimeInput.max

DatePicker (now DateInput):
  - min_date -> DateInput.min
  - max_date -> DateInput.max

NumberInput:
  - min_value -> min
  - max_value -> max

Slider:
  - range (removed)
  - New properties: min, max
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Stack Trace Dialog Timeout Error Resolution
DESCRIPTION: Resolves an `asyncio.TimeoutError` that was previously raised when the stack trace dialog was displayed. This ensures a smoother user experience when debugging.

TITLE: Drawing Eyes with Toga Canvas
DESCRIPTION: This Python code snippet demonstrates how to draw two circular eyes on a Toga Canvas. It utilizes the `fill` context to color the eyes white and the `arc` method to define their shape and position. The `math.pi` constant is used for precise arc definition.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/tutorial/tutorial-4.rst#_snippet_0

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
def draw_eyes(self):
    with self.canvas.fill(color=WHITE) as eye_whites:
        eye_whites.arc(58, 92, 15)
        eye_whites.arc(88, 92, 15, math.pi, 3 * math.pi)
```

TITLE: Toga 0.4.9 Release Notes
DESCRIPTION: Release notes for Toga version 0.4.9. This release primarily adds an upper version pin to Toga's Travertino requirement to prepare for upcoming breaking changes in Travertino.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_42

LANGUAGE: text
CODE:
```
0.4.9 (2025-02-07)
==================

This release contains no new features. The primary purpose of this release is to add an upper version pin to Toga's Travertino requirement, protecting against the upcoming Toga 0.5.0 release that will include backwards incompatible changes in Travertino.  (`#3167 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/3167>`__)

Bugfixes
--------

* The testbed app can now be run on *any* supported Python version. (`#2883 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2883>`__)
* App.app is now set to an initial value of ``None``, before an app instance is created. This avoids a potential ``AttributeError`` when the test suite finishes. (`#2918 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2918>`__)

Misc
----

* `#2476 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2476>`__, `#2913 <https://github.com/beeware/toga/issues/2913>`__
```

TITLE: Toga App.exit() Behavior Change

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_196

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:

app = App(...)

# This will now always exit the application, regardless of on_exit handler
app.exit()
```

TITLE: Toga Android WebView DOM Storage
DESCRIPTION: Enables DOM storage for WebView components on Android.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_64

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

class AndroidWebViewApp(toga.App):
    def startup(self):
        self.main_window = toga.MainWindow(title=self.formal_name)
        
        # WebView on Android will now have DOM storage enabled
        webview = toga.WebView()
        self.main_window.content = toga.Box(children=[webview])
        self.main_window.show()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    app = AndroidWebViewApp('AndroidWebViewApp', 'org.example.androidwebviewapp')
    app.main_loop()

```

TITLE: RGB and HSL Class String Representations
DESCRIPTION: The rgb and hsl classes in Toga provide string representations that are compatible with modern CSS syntax. The rgb class offers a straightforward update, while the hsl class's string representation corrects color rendering issues when using the web backend.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/changes/3611.bugfix.rst#_snippet_0

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
class RGB:
    def __init__(self, r, g, b):
        self.r = r
        self.g = g
        self.b = b

    def __repr__(self):
        return f"RGB({self.r}, {self.g}, {self.b})"

    def __str__(self):
        return f"rgb({self.r}, {self.g}, {self.b})"

class HSL:
    def __init__(self, h, s, l):
        self.h = h
        self.s = s
        self.l = l

    def __repr__(self):
        return f"HSL({self.h}, {self.s}, {self.l})"

    def __str__(self):
        return f"hsl({self.h}, {self.s}, {self.l})"
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Toga Asynchronous Task Garbage Collection Protection
DESCRIPTION: Protects asynchronous tasks from garbage collection while running, preventing unexpected termination.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_66

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga
import asyncio

async def long_running_task():
    print("Task started...")
    await asyncio.sleep(5) # Simulate work
    print("Task finished.")

class AsyncTaskApp(toga.App):
    def startup(self):
        self.main_window = toga.MainWindow(title=self.formal_name)
        self.main_window.show()

        # Start the async task, it should be protected from GC
        asyncio.create_task(long_running_task())

if __name__ == '__main__':
    app = AsyncTaskApp('AsyncTaskApp', 'org.example.asynctaskapp')
    app.main_loop()

```

CODE:

# Toga's internal imports have been updated to reflect this change.
```

TITLE: Color Validation (RGBA and HSLA)
DESCRIPTION: Implements color validation within RGBA and HSLA constructors to ensure that color values are within valid ranges. This prevents errors related to invalid color formats.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/travertino/CHANGELOG.rst#_snippet_18

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
class Color:
    @staticmethod
    def rgba(r, g, b, a=1.0):
        # Validate r, g, b, a
        if not (0 <= r <= 255 and 0 <= g <= 255 and 0 <= b <= 255 and 0 <= a <= 1.0):
            raise ValueError("Invalid RGBA values")
        # Return color object
        pass

    @staticmethod
    def hsla(h, s, l, a=1.0):
        # Validate h, s, l, a
        if not (0 <= h <= 360 and 0 <= s <= 100 and 0 <= l <= 100 and 0 <= a <= 1.0):
            raise ValueError("Invalid HSLA values")
        # Return color object
        pass
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Update Validator Behavior and Argument Naming
DESCRIPTION: The 'Contains' validator no longer supports multiple substring matches. The 'TextInput.validate' method has been removed, as validation now occurs automatically. Validator argument names have been standardized, with error messages ending in '_message', 'compare_count' renamed to 'count', and 'min_value'/'max_value' renamed to 'min_length'/'max_length'.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/background/project/releases.rst#_snippet_25

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Contains Validator:
  Multiple substring matches: removed.
TextInput:
  Method 'validate()': removed. Validation is now automatic on 'value' or 'validators' property change.
Validator Argument Renames:
  Error messages: now end with '_message'.
  'compare_count': renamed to 'count'.
  'min_value': renamed to 'min_length'.
  'max_value': renamed to 'max_length'.
```

TITLE: Toga Pack CSS Default Styles
DESCRIPTION: These are the default CSS styles applied to the reference HTML structure for Toga Pack layout testing. They ensure that the root elements behave correctly within the flexbox model.

}
```

TITLE: Toga Event Handler Deprecation: Generators to Async Coroutines
DESCRIPTION: The use of generators as event handlers in Toga is now deprecated. Developers should convert generator-based handlers to asynchronous coroutines by using `async def` and `await asyncio.sleep(t)` instead of `yield t`. This change aligns Toga with modern asynchronous programming practices in Python.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_32

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
APIDOC:
  Event Handlers: Generators -> Async Coroutines

Description:
  Generator-based event handlers are deprecated in favor of asynchronous coroutines.

Details:
  - Convert generator handlers to `async def` functions.
  - Replace `yield t` with `await asyncio.sleep(t)` for time-based delays.

Backward Incompatible Changes:
  - Existing generator-based event handlers will continue to function but will raise a `RuntimeWarning`.
  - Future versions may remove support for generator-based handlers.
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Canvas No Longer Implicitly a Context Object
DESCRIPTION: A Canvas is no longer implicitly a context object. The 'Canvas.context' property now returns the root context of the canvas. Operations to remove or clear drawing objects should be made on 'Canvas.context'.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/background/project/releases.rst#_snippet_46

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Canvas Context Access:
  OLD: canvas.context() # To generate empty context
  NEW: canvas.Context() # To generate empty context

Drawing Object Management:
  OLD: canvas.remove(obj) # Throws exception
  OLD: canvas.clear() # Throws exception
  NEW: canvas.context.remove(obj)
  NEW: canvas.context.clear()
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Resolve Memory Leaks in macOS Widgets
DESCRIPTION: Addresses memory leaks in macOS Icon and Image storage, as well as Table, Tree, and DetailedList widgets. These fixes improve application stability and performance on macOS.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_105

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
# Fixes for memory leaks in macOS Icon and Image storage
# Related issue: #2472

# Fixes for memory leaks in macOS Table, Tree, and DetailedList widgets
# Related issue: #2482
```

TITLE: Travertino Style Property Validation
DESCRIPTION: Defines validated properties of styles as dataclass class attributes. This enhances style definition and validation within the Travertino library.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/travertino/CHANGELOG.rst#_snippet_0

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
class MyStyle(BaseStyle):
    validated_property: int
```

TITLE: Toga Validators API
DESCRIPTION: A mechanism for validating user input against a predefined set of criteria, ensuring data integrity and correctness.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/reference/api/index.rst#_snippet_21

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Validators:
  Description: A mechanism for validating that input meets a given set of criteria.
```

TITLE: Data Source Notification Method Renaming in Toga
DESCRIPTION: The _notify() method on data sources has been renamed to notify() to reflect its status as a public API.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_175

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from toga.sources import ListSource

# Assuming 'source' is an instance of ListSource or similar
# source = ListSource()

# Previous method call
# source._notify()

# New method call
# source.notify()
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Toga Color Object Read-Only Attributes
DESCRIPTION: RGB and HSL color objects in Toga are now immutable. Attributes like 'r', 'g', 'b', 'a' for RGB, and 'h', 's', 'l', 'a' for HSL cannot be modified after the object is created. This enhances predictability and allows for performance optimizations.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/changes/3611.removal.3.rst#_snippet_0

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
from toga.colors import rgb, hsl

# RGB object is now read-only
color_rgb = rgb(1, 0, 0)
# color_rgb.r = 0.5  # This will raise an AttributeError

# HSL object is now read-only
color_hsl = hsl(0, 1, 0.5)
# color_hsl.h = 0.1 # This will raise an AttributeError
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Remove clear() Method from Text Input Widgets
DESCRIPTION: The 'clear()' method has been removed from 'MultilineTextInput', 'TextInput', and 'PasswordInput' to resolve ambiguity with the 'Widget.clear()' method. To clear content, set the 'value' property to an empty string.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/background/project/releases.rst#_snippet_24

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
MultilineTextInput:
  Method 'clear()' removed. Use 'widget.value = ""'.
TextInput:
  Method 'clear()' removed. Use 'widget.value = ""'.
PasswordInput:
  Method 'clear()' removed. Use 'widget.value = ""'.
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Toga Divider Widget on iOS
DESCRIPTION: Implements the Divider widget on iOS, providing a visual separator for UI elements.

SOURCE: https://github.com/beeware/toga/blob/main/docs/about/releases.rst#_snippet_51

LANGUAGE: python
CODE:
```
import toga

class DividerApp(toga.App):
    def startup(self):
        self.main_window = toga.MainWindow(title=self.formal_name)
        
        content = toga.Box(
            children=[
                toga.Label('Above Divider'),
                toga.Divider(),
                toga.Label('Below Divider')
            ]
        )
        self.main_window.content = content
        self.main_window.show()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    app = DividerApp('DividerApp', 'org.example.dividerapp')
    app.main_loop()

```
